  and the live desk sample's pivot is a matrix and its chart a stacked column. `bi.explore.browser_engine` and `browser_start_seconds` are gone.
- **Console: the runner (task 2).** A fork-server per console (pandas, numpy, pyarrow imported once; one forked child per call with its own session, limits,
  audit hook and scratch folder; `bi.python.fork_server`), the L4 result cache (item code, parameters and the exact masked input bytes;
  `bi.python.cache_*`), the `column` interface (`derive`) and `compute_groups`, and the Py-node endpoint the server's planner calls
  (`/internal/py/transform`, `/measure`, `/column`, `X-Drishti-Runner-Token` = `bi.python.runner_token` / `DRISHTI_BI_TRANSFORM_CONSOLE_TOKEN`).
- **Console: the calculation UI (task 3).** The model designer's **Calculations** tab (calculation groups, parameters, the business calendar, FX
  conversion) and **Quick calculation** in the Measures tab (% of total, ignoring a slicer, via a relationship, period to date, balances, yesterday, a
  what-if shock; from the function catalogue); a measure's **Via**; the ExpressionEditor completes `param.x`, `SELECTED`, `via=` ids and `ALL`; a new number
  format `share` (always a share of 1).
- **Console: live everywhere (task 4).** Reports, boards and the alerts page (a **Now** column) follow standing queries whose answers the server pushes
  over the console's one channel (`mq:<id>`); a state chip under each visual (Live, Throttled, Every few seconds, Stale, Not updating, As of, Window); a
  time picker (now, as of a date, a window) on reports, boards and Analyse; parameter slicers and *Show as* (calculation groups) on a report over a model.
- **Console: explain and usage (task 5).** *How this was answered* over each visual (tiers, layers, timings, the plan as text); **Admin → BI → BI usage**
  (questions by model, cache hit rate, layers, readers, SQL, other BI actions, the engine and the Python runner, from the audit log). Docs: EXPRESSIONS.md
  5.4 and recipes R60–R72, BI_GUIDE 25.1, OPERATIONS (the console's runner, BI usage), REPORTS, ANALYSE, PYTHON_BI, tutorials 12 and 14.

## 2.2.0 — Datasets and data models rooted in packs, a Python-like expression language, read-only SQL, one query plan (2026-10-17)

- **Server: the model layer's loose ends from the wave A console lane and its QA (wave A).** The **AI authoring hooks work on models**: Describe a
  report, Suggest visuals, Explain in plain words and the helper (was `DRS-9034`) are grounded in the model's `describe` as the author reads it, a
  draft is pinned to it (`rachana: 2`, `model: name@major`), Ask takes a model, and the model designer has **Ask the AI helper** (relationships,
  measures, `fields:` descriptions, one undoable edit each); Explain this number's lineage names the model's version and each table's dataset by its
  current name and version. **Semver:** every publish of a changed model or `rachana: 2` dataset carries a new version (one version string, one
  content); a contract that exposes less is breaking and needs a new major (`DRS-9126`); a reader pinned `@1` keeps the latest published 1.x after
  2.0.0 (its own layers). **The publish gate** runs a model's tests and a `rachana: 2` dataset's (`agg` with `expect`, `unique`, `not-null`,
  `references-exist`, as Drishti SQL on its implicit model; `DRS-9043`). **One model per name** in a workspace and in the catalogue (`DRS-9004`);
  `bi migrate --models --dry-run` finds the collisions the run finds. **Lineage and impact** of models, `rachana: 2` datasets and reports on models
  are computed from the items as they are now (`GET /items/{id}/lineage`, `/impact`); a model's consumers include notebooks bound with
  `dataset: mq:`, Python datasets' inputs and readers pinned to an earlier major. **Refresh** of a `rachana: 2` dataset (the sample Python dataset
  gave `DRS-9102`) drops its kept Transform results and rebuilds the tables of every model that reads it. The pnl-vs-exposure **slicer** under the
  report's `Trades > 0` groups its values (`DRS-9027`). **Rollups and row rules:** a dimension whose rules restrict nothing for the reader is not
  joined, and one joined only for its rules keeps the rollup when the fact joins it on a grouped column (answers equal the columns layer).
  **Hidden-for:** a hidden field or measure answers `DRS-9163` "not available to your role" on every endpoint (no raw `DRS-9020`), and Check warns
  `DRS-9132` "hidden measure still reachable through <columns>; hide the columns too". **Preview as** a role works on the owner's draft model. DEL
  names `??` (write `coalesce`), calls `c ? a : b` old syntax only when a `:` follows, and the expression editor no longer repeats a fix beside its
  button. `bi mq --sort` (`--sort=-X` descending). `POST /sql/validate` types its columns (a count is an `integer`). A migration problem shows on
  the item's own page (`migrationProblems`). Migration notes reworded. Tutorials 10 (step 1 and the wizard, step 13) and 15 (steps 4 and 8) on the
  model layer, pictures retaken.
- **Console: the Wave A experience QA fixes.** The field being typed in commits before every save (Ctrl+S, Save, Publish, a tab switch) in the model,
  report and board designers and both dataset wizards, and the Contract tab redraws without `NotFoundError`. The model designer **autosaves** the draft
  2 s after each change (*Draft saved at …*) and a stale save opens a **merge view** (theirs and yours per part; the browser journal stays the backup);
  publish refusals stay under the toolbar with the tab that fixes them; the Contract tab follows the server's semver (the proposed version, the
  refusal explained). Security: a hidden measure names the visible columns that still reach it (`DRS-9132`) with **Hide these columns too**, the text
  says what hiding does (`DRS-9163`), a **free row rule** in DEL (columns and `user.*` completed) and a **Tests designer** (expected values, tolerance,
  role); **Preview as a role** picks the measure and the grouping and shows the role's refusal. A visual hidden for the reader says *Not available to
  your role* (no Retry); an empty visual for a role without a row rule says *Your role has no rows of <dataset>* (`meta.noAccess`). Expression
  editor: fixes for an open bracket, `=` for `==` and a name not found (`DRS-9152`), never a sum over a text column; aliases in `count(`, numbers first
  in `sum(`, functions above column-matched fields; the report's own measure editor spans the pane; the wizard's SQL view gets the editor. Wizard:
  working examples (no `fx()`), **Write one** for a Python column, Preview asks before saving a draft and shows bare column names, never *nothing to
  fix* beside a problem, no pointer to the version 1 builder. Analyse ticks one fact of a two-fact model; pickers say draft / published / published with
  draft changes; the item page shows what a migration could not convert and picks audience roles from a list; *Your datasets* hides shipped samples
  (toggle). Canvas: **Tidy the layout**, no overlapping boxes, the Valid badge counts pinned warnings; the YAML tab themed with a screen-reader name;
  perspectives at the top of Fields, measure renames carried into perspectives, contract and hidden-for. Phone: 44 px targets on the BI pages, tab
  lists take ArrowLeft/Right/Home/End. Tests: `test_wave_a_qa_console_browser.py`.

- **Server: the 2.1 dataset paths are removed (wave A, task 6).** The compatibility reader is gone (a `rachana: 1` dataset is no longer read as a model:
  `DRS-9151` names `drishti.py bi migrate --models`); composed and derived datasets are removed (`dataset/Composition` and their `FrameService` code; a
  `from:` or `derived:` text parses with `DRS-9190` and is still read by the converter); `POST /api/v1/bi/datasets/{id}/query` answers `410` with the
  equivalent model query (`mq`); `POST /datasets/{id}/transform/run` is refused; the phase 0 proof of concept (`/api/v1/bi/poc`, `drishti.bi.poc`) is
  removed. The 2.1 sample dataset files are gone (their models ship), except the two live ones, whose reports stay on them until W3; the sample notebook
  reads `mq:trading-risk@1` and the sample Transform's inputs are the Python dataset's (`pnl`, `exposure`).
- **Server: what the wave A console lane found (wave A).** DEL `round(x, n)` runs (the digits cast to INTEGER; recipe R16). A dataset's `where` with
  `group` filters the rows before they are grouped (it was applied to the groups: `DRS-9020`). A reader of a published report opens it and its envelope
  without read on the model (`GET /reports/{id}` describes the model within the envelope, `envelope: true`). `POST /bi/models/{ref}/preview-as {role,
  query}` answers as that role would get it (bi-admin or the model's owner; audited `bi-model-preview-as`). `POST /bi/expr/validate` takes the designer's
  unsaved model or dataset (`yaml`) and flags a measure a model query would refuse (`DRS-9156`) while it is written. A report's own measures
  (`measures:`) are checked (`DRS-9154`) and sent as `define` by every visual the server draws; `perspective:` narrows the described model. StableIds
  writes `{ id: r_1, from:` with its space. A Python dataset is as of the date its inputs were answered at, as its model is. A join over a window of
  several business dates matches the date too (a fact row met its dimension once per date and was counted that many times). A data-load signal
  clears and rebuilds a published model's layers for the date, as it does a 2.1 dataset's (a model kept answering from the rows before the load).
- **Server: the dataset-to-model converter, and the shipped samples converted (wave A, task 6).** `drishti.py bi migrate --models DIR [--dry-run]
  [--out D] [--keep-old] [--report F]` (`POST /api/v1/bi/migrate/models/convert`) turns every `rachana: 1` dataset into one dataset per table
  (`<old>-<table>`, its source, columns, time mode, refresh, masks and row rules) and a model of the old name (tables as aliases, so fields read as
  before); composed datasets fold into the model that reads their sources (`desk-pnl-vs-exposure` into `trading-risk`, `joined.*` and `as` names
  renamed in the reports, a left join kept as `Trades > 0`), derived ones become a Python dataset and a model; reports are rebound (`rachana: 2`,
  `model: <name>@<major>`, comments kept, same-model `links:` removed). Row rules move to the datasets; a role restricted on one table is never widened
  silently (`may-see-all` noted for the shipped samples, `False` kept for stored items). The same runs at start for stored items (the dataset item
  becomes the model, same id; audited; history kept) and by `drishti.py bi migrate --models [--dry-run]` (`POST /api/v1/bi/migrate/models`). Every shipped
  sample is converted under `config/bi/models` and `config/bi/datasets` and proven to answer as the 2.1 dataset did for risk, a desk-head and a reader
  without raw (`ConvertedSamplesTest`, BI_MODEL_SPEC.md 8.3.1 and 8.6). The two live reports stay on their live datasets until W3; the 2.1 dataset files
  stay until the removal commit. Report YAML accepts `rachana: 2` (a report on a model).
- **Server: stored items in the old expression syntax are converted at start (wave A, task 6).** So that refusing the old syntax breaks nothing a person
  saved, the server converts every stored BI item (draft, proposal and published text) to DEL v2 once at start (`drishti.bi.migrate.on-start`, default
  on; idempotent): the text before is a saved state of the item's history (restorable), a changed published text becomes the next published version, a
  draft stays a draft, and each change is an item event and an audit record (`bi-expressions-migrated`, `bi-migration-run`). An item the converter cannot
  convert is never changed: it is listed in Admin, BI settings (`migrationProblems`), on its page and at `GET /api/v1/bi/migrate/problems`, and logged.
  `POST /api/v1/bi/migrate/expressions` (`dryRun`) and `drishti.py bi migrate --expressions [--dry-run]` (no FILEs: the stored items; bi-admin).
- **Console: the languages in the designers (wave A, console lane).** One **expression editor** for every DEL expression (a model's measures, a report's
  own measures, the dataset wizard's filter and worked-out columns, the guided builder's formulas): completion of fields, `measure('…')` and the
  server's functions (signature, example, a link to the guide), keyword arguments, the server's problems as you type with the fix as a button, *Try it*
  on the saved draft, measure chips. The **dataset wizard v2** (`/bi/datasets/new-layer`): a column subset, a row filter, what one row is with an
  explicit de-duplication (latest or earliest by a column), formula and Python columns, a grouping, a server preview, and SQL views over a model.
  The **model designer** gains a *Fields* tab (display name, description, format, folder, hidden, sort by, data category, default summarisation),
  perspectives, `hidden-for`, *Preview as a role*, the contract diff against the published version with the semver bump in one click and the consumers
  it affects; a save on a published model shows *Draft changes (v1 published)* and offers Publish again; its YAML is a minimal patch of the saved text
  (comments, key order and untouched entries kept). A report on a model inherits the model's field properties (display names in every label, folders,
  hidden fields) and perspectives, has its own measures (`measures:`, sent as `define`), and shows a visual's question as MQ or SQL with *Run as SQL*.
  Analyse SQL cells run Drishti SQL on the server over the extract's model by default (over the extract stays selectable until wave B). The alert cell
  chooser sends `reduce` only when chosen. Guides: [EXPRESSIONS.md](docs/guides/EXPRESSIONS.md) section 11 (59 finance and risk recipes, each run as a
  test on the demo lake) and 8.1 (the editor), DATASETS.md section 15, REPORTS.md section 19, BI_GUIDE.md section 24, ANALYSE.md section 4.
- **Console: off the 2.1 dataset paths (wave A, task 6).** Every read is a model query (`/bi/mq`) or Drishti SQL (`/bi/sql`): Analyse's extracts
  (shape rows), a dataset page's preview, a filter's value list in the designer and Calc's `drishti_bi.query` (it now sends a model query; `model_ref`
  says which model a name stands for). A source written before 2.2 (`file:dataset:trades-basic`, or a bare dataset name) reads the model the converter
  made of it (`trades-basic@1`), so an old notebook's SQL cells run on the server too. Promote to a report writes a `rachana: 2` report on that model and
  matches the model's measures; a slice of another model is not added to an existing report (`DRS-9070`). The phase 0 proof of concept is removed from
  the console (`/bi/poc`, `bi.poc_enabled`, its page, script, styles, routes, the `poc:rows` channel and its tests); the dataset page's *Run the
  Transform now* is gone with derived datasets. With the 2.1 dataset paths removed on the server: the server draws every visual of a report on a model
  (the console no longer builds its model queries, so carried values, report filters, dates and the report's own measures are the server's; the Query
  panel shows the model query the server ran); an old /bi/d/ link to a model-layer dataset opens its page, which gains Refresh (the state, Refresh now,
  Set a schedule, written into the draft with comments kept) and Expected values; the model designer's Contract tab runs the model's tests; a table
  visual's key opens its entity in the terminal again (the console reads each table's pack kind); the designer's picker lists only version 1 datasets
  under *Other datasets* and offers *Also add* only for those; a model line with a comment is read; Promote to a report reads a Drishti SQL cell
  (aliases and MEASURE('...')); the AI draft and suggest ask with the report's model; refusals passed through the proxy keep their `mq`, problems and fixes.
- **Tutorials on the model layer (wave A).** Tutorial 11 (BI that runs itself) on a dataset and a model of the model layer: the dataset's page refreshes it
  (one line for a one-table dataset: its schedule and its last run, whoever started it), an alert on a measure of a model beside the one on a report's
  visual, a report's tests that stop a publish and a model's own tests (Drishti SQL) run as a role, with UI, command line and YAML in every step and the
  pictures retaken. New commands: `drishti.py bi item new|save|tests` (items from files, their expected values; exit 1 on a failure) and
  `drishti.py bi dataset refresh [--now]`; `bi model list` prints a model's name@major. Tutorial 12 (live BI and boards) says what stays on the version 1
  live datasets until W3 and what the model the converter made of them answers (a model query over the live table and its kept groups, with the SQL);
  `bi mq` prints the layer, the rollup and, with `options: { explain: true }`, the SQL; `bi catalogue get` spaces its columns. Tutorial 13 (Govern BI) on a dataset and a
  model: history, review, certification, the model's lineage (what each alias reads and who reads the model, now on its item page) and its contract (a field
  taken out of what consumers may use is a major change with the reports that use it named; listing what consumers may use starts from everything), the
  dataset's DRS-9126, bundles and roll-back; renames stay a version 1 dataset feature. The console passes `/lineage` on, and the impact box is not shown
  on a dataset of the model layer (the model's Contract tab says it). Tutorial 14 (Python and Analyse) on the model: the Python measure in the model,
  Analyse's extract as one model query, SQL cells in Drishti SQL on the server (the extract engine until wave B), the sample's Python dataset over two model
  queries, promoting into a report on the same model, Calc's drishti_bi on a model; a Python measure in an alert is refused with DRS-9162. The console's
  promoted Transform says how to make a Python dataset, a promoted slice is titled by the model, a Python or model dataset's page has no Refresh, and `bi
  model describe` shows a Python measure's item. Tutorial 15 (AI) on the model: Ask the data and Explain this number on the model
  world-economics@1 with the same question as a model query; the authoring hooks (Describe, Suggest, the dataset helper) on the World economics dataset in
  its 2.1 form (docs/tutorials/data/world-economics-21.dataset.yaml), since in 2.2 they answer DRS-9034 on a model. Tutorial 16 (embed) shows a report on a
  model in the sample host (/report?report=...&visual=...) with the model query the server ran for a visual. Drishti Elements 1.4.1: a slicer in an
  embedded report asks for its values as the console does (a list or dropdown slicer used to fail the whole report with "Cannot read properties of
  null"), and a report with no live table has no live bar. Tutorial 17 (production) for 2.2: the backup holds config/bi/models/, and
  upgrading to 2.2 converts stored BI items once at start (the file and stored dry runs of `bi migrate --models`, the migration in an item's History);
  a rollback from 2.2 needs the backup.
- **Server: the old expression syntax is refused (wave A, task 6).** `drishti.bi.expr.legacy` now defaults to `refuse`: an expression in the old syntax
  is `DRS-9100` with the DEL v2 text as the fix (`drishti.py bi migrate --expressions` converts files; `legacy: warn` reads it while a site converts its
  own items). A text that is already DEL v2 but wrong gets its DEL v2 problem, and a rule written as a YAML boolean (`where: true`) reads as `True`.
  Every shipped sample, tutorial dataset and test fixture is in DEL v2.
- **Server: model semantics and access (wave A, task 5).** Models carry field properties every consumer inherits (`fields: { alias.column: { display,
  description, format, folder, hidden, sort-by, category, summarise } }`, and `display`/`folder`/`hidden` on measures), perspectives (`perspectives:`;
  `describe?perspective=`), and object-level security (`security.hidden-for: { roles, fields, measures }`): a hidden field or measure, and every measure that
  reads a hidden column, is absent from `describe`, DEL and SQL and is `DRS-9152` "not found" in a model query. A published report's readers without read
  on its model ask within the report's **envelope** (`DRS-9151` "this report cannot ask that" outside it); `GET /bi/reports/{id}/envelope`,
  `GET /bi/models/{id}/impact?target=`. Full breaking-change detection: a measure whose meaning changes, a field removed or retyped, a grain change
  (`DRS-9126` without a new major).
- **Server: dataset derivation and roots (wave A, task 4).** A dataset that `extends` another may keep a subset of its columns (`columns: [a, b]`, keys
  kept), filter (`where`), keep one row per key (`grain: { one-row-per, keep: latest by updatedAt }`, never a silent distinct), derive DEL columns
  (`derive: { x: { expr, type } }`), read a field one hop along a pack reference (`related(desk.name)`), add Python columns through the existing Transform
  runner (`derive: { x: { python, inputs, type } }`, the base's rows asked as the reader), and group to a declared coarser grain (`group: { by, columns }`).
  The plan shows `Dedup`, `Derive` and `Regroup` above the scan; a column derived from a masked one is masked; shaped datasets never route to a rollup.
  Check traces every dataset to its root (`DRS-9110` when it cannot) and answers `lineage`; `GET /api/v1/bi/lineage/{ref}` for a report, model or dataset.
- **Server: Drishti SQL (wave A, task 3).** Read-only SQL over one model (`bi/sql`): a hand-written recursive-descent parser for the documented
  subset (Calcite 1.40's dependencies are not all in the offline cache, and the dialect's table clauses are its own), a binder that resolves names as the
  caller sees the model, MQ-shaped statements compiled to exactly one model query (same plan hash, same cache entry as the MQ; inline aggregates become DEL
  local measures), composite statements (`WITH`, `UNION`, subqueries, windows over the answer) run as model-query leaves plus relational work in an
  in-memory DuckDB that can read nothing else (a join or sort on a masked column `DRS-9161`), one time clause per statement (`AS OF DATE`, `FOR LIVE`,
  `FOR WINDOW`), `EXPLAIN`. `POST /api/v1/bi/sql` (`json`, `csv`, `arrow`), `POST /api/v1/bi/sql/validate`; SQL view datasets (`source: { sql, model }`,
  asked as each reader); a model's `tests: [{ sql, expect }]`; `drishti.py bi sql`. Codes `DRS-9170` to `DRS-9179`; settings `drishti.bi.sql.*`;
  new guide [SQL.md](docs/guides/SQL.md).
- **Server: the logical plan (wave A, task 2).** Every question (MQ, a report visual, an alert, an Analyse slice; Drishti SQL next) becomes one plan
  (`bi/plan`: `SecureScan`, `SemiJoin`, `Filter`, `Aggregate`, `Meet`, `Window`, `Having`, `Sort`, `Limit`, `Answer`; `Derive`, `Dedup`, `Regroup`, `Rel`
  reserved for the dataset derivation and composite SQL). The security rewriter runs before the optimiser (predicate pushdown, top-N as a grouped
  sub-plan, projection pruning, rollup matching only when the rollup holds the row rules' columns); lowering hands the SQL compiler the same request as
  before. `meta.plan` (plan hash: one per question, any language), `meta.signature` (the reader's effective security, opaque), `options.explain` gives
  `meta.explain` (rules counted, never shown). An **L1 result cache** keyed by plan, signature and generation: readers with identical security share
  answers (`drishti.bi.plan.*`). The guards refuse from A: sums across currencies `DRS-9158`, balances added across dates `DRS-9159` (`guards: warn`
  keeps W1). Property tests: no optimiser rule touches a scan's security (1,000 random plans), ANDing a rule never adds a row, a rollup answers as the
  columns layer.
- **Server: DEL v2, the Python-like expression language (wave A, task 1).** Measures, derived columns, filters and row rules are written in a safe subset
  of Python's expression grammar (`sum(trades.mtm, where=trades.status == 'Live')`, `measure('P&L 1d') / measure('Exposure (EE peak)')`, `a if c else b`,
  `x is None`), parsed by one hand-written parser on the server (`bi/expr`), checked against its scope (row, rule, aggregate), types and a function
  catalogue (`bi/expr/functions.yaml`, 59 functions; wave B's refused with `DRS-9129`), and lowered to the same compiler tree as before, so the SQL is
  unchanged. New: `endswith`, `week`, `date('…')`, `timestamp('…')`, `days_between`, `business_days_between`, `//`, `**`, chained comparisons;
  `/` `//` `%` give `None` on a zero divisor. `GET /api/v1/bi/expr/functions`, `POST /api/v1/bi/expr/validate` (as the caller: masked `DRS-9161`,
  hidden `DRS-9152`, fixes and positions), `POST /api/v1/bi/expr/convert` (one expression, or a whole item's YAML with comments kept). The old syntax is
  read with a `DRS-9100` warning (`drishti.bi.expr.legacy: warn`; `refuse` at the end of A; `expressions: 2` on an item refuses it now); every shipped
  expression converts to identical SQL (tested). CLI: `drishti.py bi expr functions|validate|convert`, `drishti.py bi migrate --expressions`. New guide
  [EXPRESSIONS.md](docs/guides/EXPRESSIONS.md) (its function reference is generated from the catalogue; a test keeps them equal). New codes `DRS-9127`,
  `DRS-9129`, `DRS-9190` in `ErrorCode`.
- **Server: the W1 QA findings.** A model **rows** extract (`shape: rows`, Analyse, *Show the rows*) keeps one row per fact row (it was `DISTINCT`: 9 rows
  for 60 trades); its sums equal the measures. **Make it a model** reads the designer's quoted `model: "dataset:..."`, reuses a model already written out
  from the dataset in the workspace, and lists the other reports on the dataset to move. **Models are shared by reference**: `GET /bi/models/{ref}/consumers`
  (and `usedBy` in `describe`) lists reports, boards, notebooks, model-answer datasets, alerts and live subscriptions; a model item is not deleted while
  anything reads it (`409 DRS-9004`); `/mq` and report publish need read on the model, and `dataset:<item>` needs read on the dataset item. A **narrowed
  dataset** reads as its base (Check returns the base's columns and grain; `GET /bi/layer-datasets/{id}` gives the folded YAML) and drafts are listed
  under *Your datasets*. Check warns **`DRS-9131`** naming the roles a model's row rules leave out; their answer says *you have no access to rows of
  <alias> in this model* (`meta.noAccess`, `X-Bi-No-Access`); `GET /bi/models/roles` feeds the role picker. Default measures format amounts `amount2` and
  counts `integer`. Alerts refuse a number masked for their owner and a many-row visual or query without a cell or `reduce`. A masked cell reads `•••`
  in CSV, Excel, PDF and mail too. Pack dataset cards carry the kind's description.
- **Console: the W1 QA findings.** Analyse reads **models and pack datasets** (a grouped *Model or dataset* picker; extracts and slices go through the model
  query, shape rows; promoting a model slice to a report says it is not available yet). Alerts get a **measure of a model** source with pickers (model, measure,
  filters; no typed ids), the visual source asks which column and which row or aggregate to watch, and a masked number cannot be saved (Save looks first and says
  why). The model designer: Publish returns for a newer saved version of a published model, a private-workspace model shows Publish disabled with the reason and
  **Move to a workspace**, the security rule has a **Roles** picker (`GET /bi/model-api/roles`) with *roles without a rule see no rows*, the header agrees with the
  Security tab, a fresh canvas shows hints not errors, the Datasets list leaves the Security, Measures and Contract tabs, and **Used by**, **New report on this
  model** and **New board on this model** make plain that a report references a model and never owns it (`GET /bi/model-api/consumers`, `POST
  /bi/model-api/rebind`; **Make it a model** offers to move the other reports on the dataset; a used model cannot be deleted, a report's deletion never touches its
  model; a report-only measure is labelled with **Move to the model**). The Fields pane lists Measures first and says whether a measure is a default or the model's.
  One **New dataset** entry (the guided tables builder is linked from its first step), the wizard fits 390 px and its column table is readable (base columns greyed,
  *Adds up* no for text, *One row per* preselects the base key); the designer's start and *New item* offer models and pack datasets in groups; the Models page lists
  your models and datasets first and the packs' datasets in collapsible groups; Subscribe's **When** is built from choices; masked matrix cells read `•••` everywhere;
  wide matrices scroll inside their visual; a Check on a board clears when the board changes; a non-member is told so. Docs: BI_GUIDE section 23, tutorial 10.

- **Changed: every BI consumer asks through the model query language** (wave W1 packages S4 and S5, [BI_MODEL_SPEC.md](docs/architecture/BI_MODEL_SPEC.md)
  section 5.9). Report visuals (and with them exports, subscriptions, board and live tiles, the embedded report element), Analyse extracts (`POST
  /datasets/{id}/query`), alerts, dataset tests and AI ask/explain become model queries on the dataset's model and run through the one validator and compiler, as the
  reader; the endpoints keep their answers and codes (a refusal also carries the model query's `problems` with codes, paths and fixes), and the report query answer adds
  `mq`, the query it ran. Alerts get a `model` source (`{type: model, model, query, reduce?}`: a model query that selects one measure). A `rachana: 2` dataset whose
  source is another model's answer is now read (the inner query as the reader, staged for the one query); a model that reaches itself that way is refused (`DRS-9109`),
  an answer without a declared column is `DRS-9108`. Connector-query and Python-Transform datasets answer too (the SQL in its
  SQL-capable connector with only the named dates bound, `DRS-9102` when it cannot run; the Transform over its input model queries as the reader). **Reports
  bound with `model:`** are read by every report endpoint (validate, query, publish, exports, subscriptions, boards, embeds); publishing one needs its model
  published and passing its Check and safeguard (`DRS-9030` otherwise). `GET /bi/models` lists the caller's draft models with `draft`, `owner`, `workspace`.
  Fixed: a model source's `query:` was taken for a connector query. The W1 acceptance test (a report, an alert
  and an Analyse slice give the same masked answers per reader) and standing-query parity are in `ModelAcceptanceTest`.

- **Added: the BI model layer, wave W1 console package** ([BI_GUIDE.md section 23](docs/guides/BI_GUIDE.md#23-models-and-pack-datasets-the-model-layer-wave-w1)).
  **Models** in the BI rail: the pack-generated datasets (Use starts a report on the implicit model at once), your datasets and the models; a dataset page with
  its grain, keys, meanings, time mode and security. **The model designer** (`/bi/md/<item>`): datasets on a canvas with inferred star roles, relationships
  suggested from the packs' entity references (one click; a second path or a fact-to-fact one offered inactive), relationship inspector, measures with the right
  defaults from the column (aggregation, format, additivity, currency), security rules built without typing and **the safeguard** answered in one click (a rule,
  or an audited `may-see-all`), contract and version, Check on every edit, undo and redo, unsaved work kept in the browser, stale saves caught, Publish or
  Propose, a two-way YAML tab. **New dataset** (`/bi/datasets/new-layer`): a narrowed pack dataset, a connector query, a Python Transform or a model's answer, in
  three steps that write `rachana: 2`. **Reports on a model** (`model:`): the Fields pane reads the model, every visual asks one model query (`/mq`) in the
  designer and the viewer, *Query of the selected visual*, *Make it a model*. Analyse extracts of a model are one model query of rows. Publishing a report on a
  model waits for the server's consumer package ([REPORTS.md section 18](docs/guides/REPORTS.md#18-a-report-on-a-model-wave-w1)); tutorial 10 has a models preview.
- **Added: the BI model layer, wave W1 server package** ([BI_MODEL_SPEC.md](docs/architecture/BI_MODEL_SPEC.md)). New item kind `model` (shipped files in
  `config/bi/models/`) and `rachana: 2` datasets (one governed table: source, grain, typed columns with roles, units and additivity, time mode, own security), each with
  Check (`DRS-9100` to `DRS-9130`), stable ids written on save and semver contracts (a breaking change needs a new major, `DRS-9126`). Every pack kind publishes a
  read-only dataset `pack.<pack>.<kind>` (`GET /api/v1/bi/pack-datasets`); a query on `dataset:<name>` gets its implicit model (default measures, pack dimensions one
  hop). **The model query language v1** (`mq: 1`, YAML or JSON): `POST /api/v1/bi/mq`, `/models/{id}/query`, `/mq/validate`, `GET /models`, `/models/{id}/describe`,
  `POST /models/validate`, standing queries (`/mq/subscribe`, `/mq/stream`); ten validation steps with codes `DRS-9150` to `DRS-9162` and a `fix`; compiled to the existing
  engine (per-grain parts, fan-out refusal, relative windows, top-N, drill, having, totals by declared additivity; the currency and semi-additive guards warn in W1).
  **Security binds to each dataset** in a model (a model rule only ever removes rows; a dimension's rule reaches its facts), with **the safeguard**: a dataset no rule
  restricts, in a model where a role is restricted elsewhere, is flagged by Check and blocks the publish until it gets a rule or an audited `may-see-all` note
  (`DRS-9130`). Version-1 dataset files answer through the compatibility reader exactly as before (equivalence test over every shipped sample); `POST
  /datasets/{id}/query` is unchanged. New settings `drishti.bi.mq.*`; new codes in [API_GUIDE.md](docs/guides/API_GUIDE.md#error-code-table).

## 2.1.0 — The BI long tail, a multi-day type-ahead index, safe install and uninstall (2026-10-12)
- **BI adversarial QA, round 3 and the final pass.** *One title:* a dataset, report, board or notebook has the same title on its item, in its header and in its YAML after every save: rename the item and the YAML's `title:` follows, rename the `title:` in the YAML (designer, wizard, item Edit) and the item follows (`BiTitles`, in `updateItem` and `createItem`; Analyse opens a notebook under its item's title). *Private workspaces:* an item in *My workspace* shows **Publish** disabled with "Move it to a shared workspace to publish" and a Move button, and **Publish…** in both designers says the same in place. *Approving:* a steward outside the item's audience who approves gets "Approved and published. It is now visible to its audience; you are not in it." and a link to the inbox, not a script error; an item nobody may see shows only the not-found message. *Analyse:* fits 390 px with long notebook and report titles; opening a notebook restores the Dataset and Notebook selects and its ticked fields; the default ticks include the numeric columns (`mtm`, `pnl1d`, `notional`); a Promote answers beside its button (one message, never also at the top) with an *Open the draft* link, promoting the same cell or slice again offers **Update that draft** or **Save another**; a SQL-promoted report is titled "<dataset title> SQL" with a readable lower-case id; **Promote to a Transform** is shown disabled with "Add a second extract first". *Designers:* **Ctrl+S** saves from the YAML editors (report, board, the dataset wizard's YAML) and never opens the browser's Save dialog; *Saved as a draft* is one message beside the button; the board **Check** summary counts the draft-report warnings the panel lists (and the badge hides at zero); the board toolbar's View select is inline; the Designer's samples list boards as well as reports; a refused drop no longer marks *Unsaved changes*; *Also add* leaves out the main dataset. *Workspaces:* a new **dataset** can also be created by pasting YAML (for experts) next to *Open the dataset wizard*. *Small:* a value that rounds to zero shows `0.00`, never `-0.00`; the top bar's buttons are 44 px on a phone; the business-date box is filled on every page (a failed business-date call from the sign-in page used to be cached for a minute for everyone); the Tutorials card counts the tutorials it finds.
- **Packs, connectors and artifacts, final pass:** Admin → Packs shows *Changed* and the deploy history in the page's zone with its label (as Connectors and Artifacts do); **Unload** is disabled, with "Needed by ...: unload those first" in the row, while another pack builds on the pack (and the refusal is no longer followed by "the list is out of date"); a pack made by the New pack wizard needs `>=2.0.0` (what its features need), not the version of the server that built it; Artifacts shows a connector "with a warning" at the same level and label as Connectors; "which is not loaded (not loaded)" says it once; **Delete permanently** stays disabled until the exact name is typed.
- **BI adversarial QA, round 2:** *Refresh now* on a dataset whose data ends before the business date no longer builds an empty layer that every later query then read: it falls back to the latest date the source holds and says `warning: 0 rows for <date>; latest data <date>` (status `warning`), and a query never serves an empty layer as the latest. A double-clicked **Propose** proposes once and tells the reviewers once; **Publish is hidden and refused (DRS-9004) while an item is proposed**; a composed dataset's join that repeats rows is now an **error** (DRS-9015) unless it says `allow-repeat: true` (then a warning, and the Model view marks the join); a dataset over a connector that does not exist is "has errors". Also: the alert prefill name is inside the allowed characters and the rule shows beside the field, the alert form shows the report, page and visual titles, the range slicer shows From and To side by side and hints at an inverted range, a PDF line or area chart is drawn as lines with its table capped (`pdf-chart-rows`), Analyse SQL promote accepts implicit aliases and links the draft, converting a visual uses the styled confirm and clears format keys the new type does not read, the report's Business date shows the top bar day with a *Data as of* note, a frozen designer looks switched off and no longer says *Visual deleted*, a design error card has Retry, YAML syntax errors say the line, column and reason (not `reader`), export status messages replace each other, a certified item has no Promote, a connector due now says *retrying now*, Subscribe errors show beside the button, and the dataset page's messages sit apart from their buttons.

- **Packs and connectors, adversarial QA:** a connector that runs but is down shows *down, retrying* (and one with unreadable lines, or whose dated folders are all after today, *running, with a warning* with the sentence on the row), in the list, the page, the pack's Data source panel and the CLI; the editor shows errors beside Save and on the field, focuses it, disables Save while saving, masks secret fields, says when Test did not try, hides Test and Save on History and shows history times in the page's zone; one way out for a file connector (Uninstall, restorable); Admin → Packs and Connectors poll every 5 seconds and offer Refresh; Sutra problems with file:line on the pack row and page; `pack.yaml` syntax errors say line and column and are not retried; versions are X.Y.Z in the wizard, `pack bundle|make` and the deploy check; no duplicate kept version on a same-version redeploy and no rollback to the running one; the packs list names template and route connectors and fills Changed; Switch off, Unload, Load and Deploy use the styled dialog; a configuration-named pack can only be disabled (said up front); the wizard keeps its work across Back and reload and defaults JSON Lines kinds to the File connector; Admin lists are cards on a phone with the intro folded; each Admin page tells a non-admin its own sentence; an explicitly empty `packs: []` means no packs (documented).
- **Fixed:** an Analyse Python cell on the server or in the browser threw "show is not a function" (the SQL result drawer shadowed the Python one); the admin BI file links are 44 px on touch; the region-economy sample documents carry a `retrievedAt` (the compile date of the illustrative set); browser tests follow the Chart page, the last-hour line, the rollup name in the live footer and the Vega-Lite visual.
- **Added: tutorial 18, Several datasets in one report** (`docs/tutorials/18-several-datasets-in-one-report.md`, 21 pictures from `tools/docs/shots/t18.py`, seven data files in `docs/tutorials/data/`): a report over two datasets with links (a slicer and a click carry across, an unlinked country does not, a wrong ISO-3 to ISO-2 link gives an empty visual), the inflated total of a row join (P&L 5.1 times, exposure 42.8 times) against per-grain sums, a composed dataset with its `DRS-9014`/`DRS-9015` checks, the same as a Python Transform, two readers (rita, hana) filtered before the join, the join in Analyse promoted to a visual or a Transform, and an honest Power BI comparison. All numbers and outputs were captured from a real stack.
- **Added and fixed: the board designer, "view as readers see it", visual ids, feedback beside controls and Back after sign-in (adversarial QA, deferred items).** (1) The board designer's report list includes **your own draft reports**, marked *(draft)* (`GET /bi/boards/reports`); their tiles draw for the author, say so on their face, and **publishing or proposing a board that names a draft report is refused** (`DRS-9052`, naming the report; force does not help); the same visual added twice warns beside *Add tile* instead of blocking; Check on a board with no tiles says **"add a tile"** in plain words (the empty-page problem is now `this page has no tiles yet: add a tile ...`); dragging a tile by its header or corner keeps the Column/Row/Wide/High fields in step (a browser test drags both). (2) A **View** menu in both designers: *my draft* or *as readers see it (published)*, greyed until published; `GET /bi/items/{id}/published` and `?as=published` read the published version of what a call opens, with the caller's own masks, and the page shows a banner with the version. (3) A **visual id follows its type** on conversion for any made-up id (`card-1`, `slicer-1`, `visual-3`) and the bookmarks, phone and tablet layouts and `tests:` that named the old id follow it (Undo restores both). (4) **Feedback beside the control** for the catalogue and home favourite star, item page actions (propose, publish, approve, reject, withdraw, promote, certify, delete, duplicate, move), workspace delete-selected, add member and the review box, the report page's Export (now fetched, so a refusal is said beside the button), Subscribe and Alert me (a note when the screen's filters were too long to carry), a dataset page's query, refresh and schedule, and the designers' Check and Save. (5) **Back after sign-in:** `GET /login` while signed in answers `303` to `next` or home and is never cached, and the sign-in POST already answers `303`, so Back from the landing page does not show the form again; the sign-in model is unchanged. Docs: BOARDS.md 6, 7, 9, 11, REPORTS.md, BI_GUIDE.md, API_GUIDE.md.
- **Fixed and added: saving and editing safely in the BI designers and workspaces (adversarial QA).** A save names the saved state it started from (`etag`/`baseEtag`); a stale save is refused with **`DRS-9007`** (409) and the report designer, board designer, Model view, wizard in edit mode and item Edit form offer **Reload theirs / Save mine as a copy / Overwrite with mine**. **Propose, Approve and Publish refuse a report that cannot be drawn** (`DRS-9030`, errors listed; a bi-admin may force it, audited); **one bad visual is one error card** and the others draw (no page-wide `DRS-9034`). Proposed items open read-only with a banner and **Withdraw to edit**; the item page names who can approve and notifies them, hides Edit while proposed, and asks before removing a member (a warning for yourself). Designer: **+ Add visual**, palette drag onto the canvas, a confirmation before a type change drops wells, ids follow the type, **Publish/Propose** as a real action, a collapsible Filters pane, phone panes as tabs and an icon toolbar, double-click a page tab to rename, YAML errors block leaving the YAML tab, a copied sample drops its licence header, a new filter starts on the selected visual's field. Dataset wizard: history per step, a leave guard, a draft kept in the session and restored after a refresh (**Discard draft**), edit mode opens on an overview with jump buttons, errors with a jump link to their step, a sensible default workspace. New report forms take a **Main dataset** plus **Also add**, list unusable datasets greyed with the reason and put **Add dataset** at the top of the Fields pane (`GET /bi/datasets?drafts=1`). Shared helpers put feedback beside the control and disable buttons while a request is in flight; Subscribe keeps the BI links and searches recipients; the shipped workspace no longer shows the server path. [BI_GUIDE](docs/guides/BI_GUIDE.md), [REPORTS.md section 9 and 16](docs/guides/REPORTS.md), [BI_GOVERNANCE.md section 3](docs/guides/BI_GOVERNANCE.md).
- **Added: the dataset wizard, so a business user builds BI without typing YAML.** **Datasets → New dataset (guided)** (`/bi/datasets/new`, also behind My workspace → New item → dataset and the designer's empty state) walks a source (the packs' kinds, **a connector** and its kinds, combined datasets, or a Python Transform), the columns (tick, rename, type, key, description), relationships (the packs', value-matched and same-name suggestions, editable), measures (an aggregation and a column, or a formula with a list of fields to pick from and the server's **Check**), hierarchies, a refresh schedule (with row rules shown read-only) and a review of the YAML before it is saved as a draft in the workspace you choose, then **Publish it, then start a report** or **Open the Model view**. **Edit in the wizard** on a dataset's page round-trips the saved text and keeps every key the wizard has no control for; a dataset with no schedule offers **Set a schedule**. Two small console calls do the conversion (`POST /bi/wizard/yaml`, `POST /bi/wizard/parse`). [DATASETS.md section 12](docs/guides/DATASETS.md#12-build-a-dataset-in-the-browser-the-wizard), tutorial 10 step 13.
- **Added: designer controls that needed YAML.** A slicer's Format tab has the **relative date** mode with **Starts as** and **Windows it offers**; **Bookmarks** save the page, its filters and a choice for each slicer and can be renamed, updated from the page's filters and removed; the top-N filter and the Phone and Tablet arrangements were already controls and are now documented as such.
- **Added: the workflow gaps, in the UI.** New item **python** is a form (a measure, a visual or a Transform) that creates a valid, commented starter body and opens its editor; the designer's start page chooses the workspace of a new report or board, lists drafts across every workspace where you may add items, lists the shipped reports with **Copy and design**, and **Save a copy** asks the workspace; every item page has **Duplicate...** into a workspace you choose; **Delete**, on an item page and in the workspace list, says what depends on the item first (`GET /api/v1/bi/items/{id}/dependants`: reports on a dataset, boards on a report); **Add member** searches known people; Export and Subscribe on a report appear when they work and say why when they do not (`GET /api/v1/distribution/status`).
- **Added: delete from the workspace list, and a New item form that knows what each kind needs.** The Items table of every workspace page has a checkbox for each item you may delete (`can.delete`), Select all, **Delete selected (N)** with one confirmation listing the titles, and a Delete in each row; items are deleted one by one by the ordinary rule and the message names those deleted and those skipped with the server's reason. Rows show a short id and the created time (`createdAt` is now in the item list answer) and a title left empty becomes *Report 2026-10-12 11:32* (by kind) instead of *My report*. A **report** made from the form asks for its dataset and opens in the designer; a **board** opens the board designer; datasets, notebooks and Python items go to the Datasets page and Analyse; a user without `bi-author` is told to ask for it; a report draft with no `dataset:` opens with a **Choose a dataset** box. The dataset page has **New report on this dataset**.
- **Fixed: one incomplete visual no longer blanks the designer.** The canvas preview leaves visuals with an empty required well out of its question (and any other visual the server names as the problem, then asks again), the visual's `wells` are always present after loading, the Check list names the visual (title, id, number and page), and a drop says where it fell: a field already in a table is no longer called a misfit, and a field that fits no well names the wells. The published report view is unchanged.
- **Added: several datasets in the designer.** Fields pane **Add dataset** with a collapsible tree for each, a dataset tag on a visual that reads another dataset, a field of another dataset refused in a visual that already reads one (with the hint), a **Links** panel that suggests same-name or same-meaning fields (tick to write `links:`, remove from the list) and **Make a composed dataset from these**, a `from:`/`join:` draft saved only on a click ([REPORTS.md section 16](docs/guides/REPORTS.md#16-reports-over-several-datasets)).
- **Fixed: Admin → Packs** wraps its long text so each pack's actions stay on screen; **Disable / Enable** on a connector shows its progress on the button while the connector stops or starts.
- **Added: Admin → Artifacts (`/admin/artifacts`) and a safe uninstall.** One list of everything installed (packs, connector files, shipped BI files) with status, origin, version and problems; an **impact preview** (packs that extend it, connectors only it uses, BI datasets, reports and boards, saved searches, monitors, alert rules, measure alerts, subscriptions, shares, workspaces, embed hosts) shared by every page; **Disable** (keep, switch off: a pack off for everyone, a connector `enabled: false`, a BI file hidden from the catalogue), **Uninstall** (refused with the impact while anything depends on it, unless the dependants are taken along and the name typed; a pack-template connector and a pack named in `DRISHTI_PACKS` are only disabled) which **moves** the artifact to `data/uninstalled/<time>-<kind>-<name>/` (`drishti.admin.uninstalled-dir`, kept `drishti.admin.uninstalled-keep` 30d, pruned daily) and lets the watchers unload it; **Restore** (refused when the name exists unless replaced) and **Delete permanently** on an *Uninstalled* tab; a BI file upload with a validation and impact dry run; every action and refusal audited (`artifact-*`). The same dialog is the *Uninstall…* button on Admin → Packs, Connectors and BI. Endpoints `/api/v1/admin/artifacts/**` (never for a token), codes `DRS-5040` to `DRS-5044`, guide `docs/admin/ARTIFACTS.md`, help topic `admin-artifacts`.
- **Fixed: a connector whose file was there at start and is then removed** is gone from Admin → Connectors, no longer listed as defined in `application.yaml` (the start-up copy of the file's settings was kept until a restart).
- **Changed: the BI documentation reads as one set.** Every "not built", "planned" and "phase N" marker for what waves A to D built is gone (BI guide, recipes, datasets, reports, boards, governance, Python, Analyse, AI, distribution, Elements, README, the implementation plan); [RUPAKA.md section 20.15](docs/architecture/RUPAKA.md#2015-as-built-at-210-and-what-is-still-not-built) is the one as-built summary and the one list of what is still not built; guides point to tutorials 10 to 17 (not to chapters of an old tutorial); the help centre's summaries name the new pages (board designer, Vega-Lite, Calc `drishti_bi`, embed elements, the type-ahead index); tutorial pictures that changed were retaken. **Fixed:** `pack make`, `pack deploy`, the generated README and the CLI and operationalising guides now say what a running server does with a pack folder copied into its packs folder (it is checked and loaded by itself, no restart).
- **Added: reload the type-ahead index (admin and automatic).** `POST /api/v1/admin/connectors/{name}/index/reload` (`{kind, date}`; date a business day or `all`) rebuilds days from the source in the background and swaps each in atomically (the old day answers until then), `GET .../index` lists kinds by days with ids, tier, bytes, build time, rebuilding and last error; one job per connector (`409 DRS-5032`), audited, admin only (`token-never`). Admin → Connectors → connector → **Index** (Reload per day, per kind, all; progress; errors) and **Reload index** in Admin → Health. `index.reload-every` (default `10m`, `0` = off, per kind) checks every past day against its recorded watermark and rebuilds only changed days (sources with no version rebuild each cycle, S3 included), staggered and sharing the manual reload's slot; Health shows last check, next check and last change. **Changed:** segment files are read with positional reads and reference-counted retirement instead of memory maps, so evicted, rolled or reloaded days free their disk at once; `index.block-cache`; `drishti.index.dir` is now a setting (`DRISHTI_INDEX_DIR`, default `./data/index`) ([OPERATIONS.md section 12.2](docs/admin/OPERATIONS.md#122-sizing-the-multi-day-type-ahead-index), [ARCHITECTURE.md section 9.1](docs/architecture/ARCHITECTURE.md#91-several-business-days)).
- **Added: multi-day type-ahead index.** `index.days`, `index.max-memory`, `index.storage`, `index.max-disk`, `index.warm`, `index.any-recent-day` (and `index.kinds.<kind>.*`) on the Delta, Iceberg, DuckDB, PostgreSQL/JDBC, file and S3 connectors keep several business days of ids: a shared id dictionary and one bitmap per day, every lookup by a concrete business date (live is resolved to the newest date; undated kinds use an explicit `UNDATED` key). Closed days can move to memory-mapped, block-compressed segment files under `drishti.index.dir` (default `<data dir>/index`, capped by `index.max-disk`, reused after a restart only while the source's watermark is unchanged, written atomically). Health and the connector page show days, ids, memory, disk and warm state ([ARCHITECTURE.md section 9.1](docs/architecture/ARCHITECTURE.md#91-several-business-days), [CONFIGURATION.md](docs/admin/CONFIGURATION.md#drishtiindex--the-multi-day-type-ahead-index), [OPERATIONS.md section 12.2](docs/admin/OPERATIONS.md#122-sizing-the-multi-day-type-ahead-index)).
- **Added: wave D, AI, distribution and embedding.** (1) **Narrative summaries:** `narrative: true` on a subscription opens each email with a short written summary of the recipient's own masked figures, only when the AI policy lets the model see values (`drishti.bi.ai.values: aggregates`), through the LLM layer as the recipient (feature `subscription-narrative`); (2) the **designer copilot** (Suggest visuals, Explain the Check panel in plain words) and (3) the **dataset helper** (relationships, measures, column descriptions), each proposing and applied only on accept (`POST /bi/ai/suggest`, `/explain-checks`, `/dataset-helper`; features `bi-suggest`, `bi-explain-checks`, `bi-helper`); (4) **native Excel charts** (bar, column, line, area, pie, donut) in `.xlsx` exports, masked columns never plotted; (5) **retries of failed alert email** by the shared policy (`drishti.retry.alerts.*`, `drishti.alerts.measure.max-attempts`), the history shows the attempts; (6) **webhook and SMS channels** for alerts and subscriptions (`drishti.notify.*`: allow-listed hosts, HMAC-signed posts, `${ENV}` secrets, TLS and proxy, retries; an HTTP SMS gateway by templates; no figure in either); (7) **embedding:** `<drishti-about>` and `<drishti-search>` (new scope `embed:search`, read-only, masked, rate-limited), Elements 1.4.0, React, Vue and Angular sample host pages. Escalation stays unbuilt. Docs: BI_AI.md 9 to 11, DISTRIBUTION.md, ELEMENTS.md, API_GUIDE.md, CONFIGURATION.md, TROUBLESHOOTING.md, AI_PROVIDERS.md, RUPAKA.md 20.14, tutorials 11, 15, 16.
- **Added: Vega-Lite visuals.** `type: vega-lite` (a specification in `format.spec`, its data the rows of its wells) and a Python visual's `output: vega-lite`, drawn with vendored Vega, Vega-Lite, vega-embed and the **vega-interpreter**, so the page keeps its strict content security policy (no `eval`); every URL and loader is refused ([REPORTS.md](docs/guides/REPORTS.md#35-vega-lite-visuals), [PYTHON_BI.md section 14](docs/guides/PYTHON_BI.md#14-vega-lite-output)).
- **Added: tests shipped with a Python item.** `tests:` (inline frames or a dataset sample, `by`, `expect`, `tolerance`; measures, visuals, transforms); Check runs them in the sandbox and a publish through the console is refused (`DRS-9076`) while one fails ([PYTHON_BI.md section 15](docs/guides/PYTHON_BI.md#15-tests-shipped-with-an-item)).
- **Added: Python on boards and in alerts.** A board tile may be a python visual (the sample board `python-wall`); an alert's measure may be a Python measure, read for each person in the sandbox on schedule or after a load, masked when a column is masked for them ([DISTRIBUTION.md](docs/guides/DISTRIBUTION.md#python-measures-in-alerts), [BOARDS.md](docs/guides/BOARDS.md#python-tiles)).
- **Added: Calc's BI client** `drishti_bi` (`query`, `datasets`, `describe`: the engine's answer as you, a DataFrame) and `drishti_bi.walk(df)`: **Graphic Walker** (PygWalker's front end, vendored; PygWalker itself cannot be vendored offline) in a sandboxed frame with a policy of its own ([PYTHON_CALC.md section 19](docs/guides/PYTHON_CALC.md#19-the-bi-client-and-graphic-walker)).
- **Added: Slice and SQL in the browser** with vendored DuckDB-Wasm in Analyse (the page keeps the strict policy; the console's engines are the fallback; `bi.explore.browser_engine`, `browser_start_seconds`), and **promotion of a slice or a SQL cell into a visual of a new or an existing report draft** with its dataset, wells and filters mapped ([ANALYSE.md sections 13 and 14](docs/guides/ANALYSE.md#13-in-the-browser-duckdb-wasm)).
- **Added: Perspective charts** (`type: perspective-chart`, the d3fc plugin) and Perspective's **settings panel** on every Perspective visual, with the view **saved back into the report's draft** as `format.view` ([REPORTS.md](docs/guides/REPORTS.md#perspective-visuals-of-21-since-230-drishtis-grid)).
- **Fixed: a measure that mixes grains** (`[MTM] / [Exposure]`) is worked out over each measure at its own grain, no longer repeating the one-side figure; the two measures of two unrelated facts in one expression are answered too ([RUPAKA.md section 20.13](docs/architecture/RUPAKA.md)).
- **Changed:** new codes `DRS-9076` and `DRS-9077`; `core/pytests.py`, `core/promote_report.py`, `bi-vega.js`, `bi-explore-duck.js`, `drishti_bi.py` and the walker frame are new console files; vendored: Vega (BSD-3), Graphic Walker with its 190 packages (Apache-2.0, MIT and others; no Hippocratic code) ([VENDOR.md](drishti-console/web/static/vendor/VENDOR.md)).
- **Added: governance beyond removal.** A field or measure renamed with the same `path:`/`expr:` is **recognised as a rename**: the impact says `[renamed to ...]` and *Publish and update the reports* (`applyRenames`) rewrites the stored reports that use the old names, as their next published version, in the same step as the dataset. A changed **type or meaning** (a column's type, path or expression, a measure's expression) is listed as an impact on the reports that use it (shown, not refused). Every import now **keeps what it replaced** (`drishti.bi.governance.imports-dir`, `imports-keep`) and **Roll back this import** (Environments; `POST /bi/imports/{id}/rollback`, audited, refused when an item was edited since unless a bi-admin forces it) puts it back. **Pack archives may carry BI content** (`config/bi/<kinds>/`): the upload check dry-runs the import, the deploy loads it as a real import into the workspace `packs` (published, the pack as audience), a refused import does not undo the deploy; a pack with no `sutras/` folder passes the Sutra checks. **Lineage reaches past the connector** to the table, topic, path or bucket its file declares (credentials never; masked for people who are not administrators). Docs: BI_GOVERNANCE.md sections 5 to 7, API_GUIDE.md, CONFIGURATION.md, TROUBLESHOOTING.md, RUPAKA.md 20.12, tutorial 13.
- **Added: history of a live table.** `live.history: { table, every, keep, by, measures }` samples measures of a live table on a timer into a bounded, time-indexed table queryable as the dataset table `history` (a `ts` column, the groups, one column per measure) with a generated measure `<measure> history` for each; the reader's masks apply on reading, a row rule it cannot honour refuses the query. The sample Live trading desk gains a last-hour line. **Tile alert hysteresis:** `alert.hysteresis` on a board tile keeps the colour (and the notification state) until the value is back inside the threshold by that much. Docs: DATASETS.md 7.1, BOARDS.md section 4, REPORTS.md, tutorial 12.
- **Added: the board designer** (`/bi/design/board/<id>`; Designer start, a board's page and its item page link to it): a 12-column grid of tiles taken from reports (drag, resize, keys), pages, the kiosk rotation, a threshold alert with hysteresis and notify for each tile, the YAML as a second view (`POST /bi/boards/format`, `validate` now returns the tiles it resolves), Save as a draft and Publish through the item page. Docs: BOARDS.md section 11, tutorial 12.
- **Added: one temp folder per process.** The server keeps `drishti-<pid>` under `drishti.tmp-dir` / `DRISHTI_TMP_DIR` and the console `drishti-console-<pid>` under `tmp_dir` / `DRISHTI_CONSOLE_TMP_DIR`; each is removed on a clean stop, stale ones of dead processes at start, and `java.io.tmpdir` / `tempfile.tempdir` point into it (DuckDB spill, native libraries, uploads, exports, the static cache, Python scratch). All temp files are made by one helper in each process (architecture and console tests forbid anything else); Admin, Health shows both folders. The static-file cache is now per process (rebuilt on start).
- **Fixed: fan-out across tables in one query.** Measures of different tables are now each aggregated at their own table's grain and joined on the query's grouping, so a one-side measure (netting-set exposure) is no longer summed once per trade when asked beside a trade measure (MTM). No shipped sample report mixes the two in one visual, so no sample figure changes. Tested by brute force on a synthetic lake and on the Trading and counterparty risk model.
- **Added: window functions in the measure language.** `lag`, `lead`, `rank`, `dense_rank`, `percent_rank`, `moving_avg`, `moving_sum`, `moving_min`, `moving_max` and `cumulative`, with an order column and optional partition columns that are fields of the visual, compiled to DuckDB window SQL over the grouped rows (so the reader's row rules apply before the window and a masked order column masks the measure); the designer's Fields pane badges them; `DRS-9013` is now only a Python measure named in a query. The Trading and counterparty risk sample gains `MTM rank` and `MTM 3-day average`. DATASETS.md 4.3.
- **Added: several unrelated fact tables in one query.** Measures that aggregate tables no many-to-one path joins are aggregated per fact at the query's fields and full-outer-joined on them, so no fact is repeated by another's rows; a field a fact cannot reach, a measure mixing facts, a window measure beside another fact and a row rule a fact cannot honour are refused. DATASETS.md 3.1.
- **Added: incremental live measures.** The rollups declared on a live table are kept by group as rows change (sums and counts by difference, a lost minimum or maximum read again for its group alone, in the batch's transaction) and a query a rollup can answer reads them (`layer: live`, `rollup` named); other measures recompute; `drishti.bi.live.incremental-aggregates`. The Live trading desk sample declares one. Tests compare it with a recompute through inserts, replacements, moves, deletes and eviction.
- **Added: refresh state and history that survive a restart,** a **Refresh now** button, the last and next run and **Recent refreshes** on the dataset page (`drishti.bi.refresh.history`, `<BI store>/refresh/state.json`; an interrupted run shows as `interrupted`, a time missed while stopped runs once at start-up).
- **Added: the relative-date slicer** (`format.mode: relative-date`: `last-N-days|business-days|weeks|months`, `this-` and `previous-` `week|month|quarter|year`, `all`; business-day aware; worked out on the server so exports and subscriptions agree); the `DRS-9033` reservation is removed.
- **Added: tablet layouts** (`layouts.tablet`: `order`, `hide`, `span`, `wide`; two columns up to 1024 px) with a **Tablet** tab in the designer; Drishti Elements library 1.3.0.
- **Added: expected values.** `tests: [{ visual | measure, filters, expect (a number or a range), tolerance, role }]` on reports and datasets; Check (the designer, the dataset page, `POST /api/v1/bi/items/{id}/tests`) runs them as the caller or a role they hold and shows pass, fail or error; **publishing refuses a failing test with `DRS-9043`** unless a bi-admin forces it (`drishti.bi.governance.refuse-failing-tests`).
- **Added: the entity-view visual** (`type: entity-view`, a `key` well): the Drishti view of the entity selected on the page, inline and built for the reader; the `DRS-9033` reservation is removed. The Counterparty risk sample has a *Counterparty lookup* page.
- **Added: the Region economics sample** (the `region-economy` kind of the economics pack, a dataset and a report that drills from the world map to Australian and US regions; **illustrative, rounded figures, labelled as such**).
- **Changed:** `GET /api/v1/bi/me` also lists the caller's `roles`; `GET /datasets/{id}/refresh` carries `history`; the dataset's live layer lists its `aggregates`.

## 2.0.0 — BI complete, maps, coherent live data, seventeen tutorials, one look (2026-10-12)
- **Changed: the documentation, consolidated for 2.0.0.** The help centre is **six sections and forty pages** instead of eight sections and ninety: *Start* (Install and run, Tutorials), *Use*, *BI*, *Data*, *Build*, *Operate*. **Install and run** merges the Quickstart, Getting started and Windows pages; **Panels** merges the panel catalogue and the panel developer guide; **Connectors** is one reference with a section per plugin (the per-plugin documents stay in the repository and open from it); **TLS and proxy** is one page; **Query by form** and **Reports and subscriptions** are pages of their own (moved out of the user guide); **BI recipes** keeps what the retired BI tutorial taught that the tutorials do not (four people, row rules and masks, the Model view); the retired Build workbench tutorial's rebase walk-through is a section of the Build workbench guide; the small console guides that repeated the user guide are gone. **Tutorials teach and guides are reference:** worked examples that a tutorial already walks through (data loads, past dates and Compare, monitors, scheduled reports) are a pointer to it. The architecture documents and runbooks leave the list (they stay in the repository, linked from `docs/README.md`). **Every retired help address redirects (301)** to its new page or section, and `/help/tutorials` renders the seventeen tutorials with their pictures. Also: the user guide's top bar, themes (Sajha's crimson, blue and green) and Live button match the code; the README feature list and *What 2.0.0 contains* in the implementation plan are current; the Admin → Connectors pictures of tutorial 4 are re-shot with a plain data folder (no scratch path). Tests: `test_help.py` (six sections, every old slug, the tutorials page), `test_help_links.py`, `test_panel_kinds.py`, `test_tutorials.py`, `test_guide_images.py`.
- **Added: BI over several datasets.** (1) **Composed datasets**: a dataset may declare `from:` (other datasets, each asked a question **as the reader**: their own row rules and masks apply *before* the join) and `join:` (`inner`, `left`, `full`, on columns of one type), plus measures of its own over the joined columns (`[Desk P&L] / [Exposure (EE peak)]`); it is used wherever a dataset is (reports, alerts, subscriptions, refresh, Analyse). Checks: `DRS-9014` (source, column, measure or key missing, keys of different types), `DRS-9015` (a join that repeats rows: a warning), `DRS-9016` (a dataset that reads itself); a join on a column a source masks for the reader is `403 DRS-9021`; what a source masks stays masked. A change to a source dataset that breaks a composed one is caught by impact analysis; lineage and the Model view (sources, join, joined table) draw it. (2) **Python Transform** (the interface of RUPAKA 13.1, server target): a Python item with `interface: transform` (`run(*frames)` over masked pandas frames, one per input dataset), published as a **derived dataset** (`derived:`; columns declared or typed from the frame, measures in YAML over it); run by the server in the console's sandboxed runner (`core/transform_cli.py` over `core/pyrunner.py`), kept per reader and version of the inputs, with row, byte, time and concurrency limits (`drishti.bi.transform.*`), `POST /api/v1/bi/datasets/{id}/transform/run`, `DRS-9075`. (3) **Reports over several datasets**: a visual's own `dataset:` and the report's `links:` carry slicers, cross-filter and carried values across datasets; each query is still run as the reader. (4) **Analyse**: a notebook loads several masked extracts (`extract("name")`, a table per extract in SQL) and promotes a join cell to a Transform draft. (5) **Samples, built both ways**: *Desk P&L vs counterparty exposure* as a composed dataset and as a derived dataset, with a report (a slicer and a donut over the second dataset) and a pandas notebook; a Chromium test compares both with an independent join. Docs: DATASETS section 11, PYTHON_BI section 13, REPORTS section 16, ANALYSE section 2, RUPAKA 20.10, tutorials 10 and 14.
- **Fixed:** an Analyse Python cell whose result held a missing value (a left merge) failed with `Out of range float values are not JSON compliant: nan`; the cell's table now shows the value as empty.
- **Changed:** one top bar everywhere: signed in, every page (the landing page, About, Help) shows the terminal's top bar; signed out, the public bar has the same height, logo and wordmark, the theme as an icon before the person.
- **Fixed:** the public pages' bar (landing, About, Help signed out) now follows the Crimson, Crimson dark, Blue and Green themes like the top bar: the theme's gradient, white text and logo, the Terminal button white with the accent as its text.
- **Added: tutorials 10 to 17** (`docs/tutorials/`): your first BI report (a map drilled from the world to a country and its regions, slicers like Power BI, publishing, Counterparty risk), BI that runs itself (a self-refreshing dataset, subscriptions with and without a mail server and how to set mail up, alerts on numbers, export), live BI and boards (coherent live data, pause and resume, the Desk wall in kiosk mode), govern BI (history, review, certify, lineage, the impact refusal, an environments dry run), Python and Analyse, AI in Drishti (the mock provider, the policy, Test connection), embedding (Admin, Embedding and the sample host under a strict CSP) and running it in production; each with real screenshots from a real stack (`tools/docs/screenshots.py --guide t10` .. `t17`), real captured output, and sample files in `docs/tutorials/data/` (a mail catcher, a mock AI provider, a Python visual, datasets and reports). Tutorials 1, 3, 7 and 9 and their pictures follow the new navigation (Forms and Reports are entries of Views); tutorial 6 has the `map` panel; the index lists all seventeen.
- **Added:** **Admin, AI, Test connection**: a button for each console provider that calls its `probe()` and `live_models()` once, on request, and shows the result inline (administrators only, one test per provider every two seconds, 15 seconds at most, the key never in the answer, every test an `ai-audit` line in the console's log). `health()` still never calls the network.
- **Fixed:** the dropdowns of a search form (trade product type, asset class) listed only the values of the first 200 rows of a kind, so a kind whose rows come grouped (the rates trades first) left the other products out; they now read up to `drishti.search.form.enum-scan-rows` (1,000, the search's own limit) rows.
- **Fixed:** a connector whose `source-name` setting is blank (a form sends one) gave its documents a blank source, so a view said "Source: , gen 0"; a blank `source-name` now means the connector's name, as documented.
- **Fixed:** disabling the development administrator now clears the "still has its default password" warning (`defaultAdminPasswordInUse` is false for a disabled account), as OPERATIONS.md said it did; the connection-pool example there adds up to 320, not 280; AI_PROVIDERS.md counts seventeen vendor provider types, not sixteen.
- **Changed:** the Trades overview sample's "By product" page has dropdown slicers for product type, desk and currency (`type: slicer`, `format.mode: dropdown`: a checkbox list with a search box, Select all and Clear), and its donut shows the twelve largest product types (`op: top`).
- **Fixed:** a dropdown slicer's list was cut off by its visual's frame; it now floats over the page while open.
- **Changed:** **Reports** is an entry of **Views** (the Reports page: every scheduled report and subscription, a box to find one, and links to your alerts, everyone's subscriptions for administrators, and the BI reports); the BI menu lists no report names (with hundreds of reports a menu cannot hold them).
- **Fixed:** on BI pages the top bar was taller and its items out of line, and the BI menu had wide gaps: the page body carried the class of BI's YAML boxes (`bi-body`, `white-space: pre-wrap`), so the HTML's line breaks showed. The body class is now `bi-page`.
- **Changed:** a view's function-key bar is one row of key chips (it scrolls sideways when it does not fit, instead of wrapping), on its own surface; its note says only when the data was updated, the generation and the date (the full source is its tooltip and in "How this view was built") and a short copyright linking to About.
- **Fixed:** the landing page's **Terminal** button read amber on amber (the links row's colour won over the button's).
- **Changed:** the top bar shows the logo and a larger wordmark only; the slogan moves to the footer, now laid out as in Sajha: one centred closing block with the slogan (Playfair, the heading colour), the product and version with Help, About, Competitive analysis and Release notes, then the fine print.
- **Changed: every live demo sample ticks as a whole.** On `/v/ir-curve/CRV-USD-BASIS` only the 10Y benchmark moved while the curve table and chart stood still, because a sample's `_meta.walk` moved only the fields it listed. The demo source now takes **coherent walks**: a group entry draws ONE random step and moves a field and every path in `with` (wildcards `points.*.zeroRate`, `history.-1.close`, selectors `points.tenor=10Y.zeroRate`; a per-path `scale` for a decimal rate against a percent one; `shape: twist` and `jitter` for a tilt and per-tenor blur; `mode: rel` for proportional moves; `min`/`max`), `derive` recomputes the dependents with a small expression language (discount factors from zero rates, `sum(legs.*.pv)`, `hqla / netOutflows`, `value >= red ? 'Red' : …`, `scaleTo` to make cashflow PVs add up to their leg), and `append` keeps a clock-keyed series current; the plain `{field: step}` form works as before, an entry that cannot be applied is skipped, and a whole number with a step of 1 or more stays whole while other values keep their decimals (so an index level or a card balance no longer rounds to an integer). `tools/packgen/common/walks.py` builds each kind's walk from the finished sample and every generator calls it, so the shipped samples carry the specs (the generators' `--check` stay clean): market-data (IR curves move as one with forwards, quotes, discount factors, 10Y headline and 2s10s; FX bid/ask/history; equity price, market cap, day bar and change; index; commodity and its curve; credit curve hazards and survival; bond price, yield, spread), trading (MTM is the sum of the leg PVs, cashflow PVs rescale to their leg, valuation, day P&L and the P&L explain keep adding up), counterparty-risk (netting-set trades, asset totals, net MTM and the exposure profile with its peaks and utilisation), banking-core books, operational-risk KRIs (status re-judged against the thresholds), retail balances (utilisation, ledger line, history), liquidity-risk (LCR from HQLA and flows, intraday peak) and the logistics container. What stays still on purpose (coupon schedules, closed statements, end-of-day risk) is listed per kind in [DEMO_DATA.md](docs/connectors/DEMO_DATA.md#live-walks-by-kind). Tests: `DemoCoherentWalkTest` (the engine), `DemoPackWalksTest` (every shipped sample ticked 40 times per kind: headline against detail, sums, ratios, df against zero rate, status against thresholds; a walked headline whose declared dependents never move, or a spec naming a path its sample lacks, fails). Docs: [DEMO_CONNECTOR.md](docs/connectors/DEMO_CONNECTOR.md#74-coherent-walks-a-group-that-moves-together) 7.4, DEMO_DATA.md.
- **Changed:** the wordmark is set in Cinzel and the tagline in Playfair Display (both SIL OFL, vendored in `static/vendor/fonts`, Latin, 38 KB), in the top bar, the public pages and the About hero; the tagline is larger and on two lines, white on the coloured bars, and the logo's eye is white there too.
- **Changed:** the Crimson, Crimson dark, Blue and Green themes are Maya's and Sajha's (Sajha's Dark is Drishti's Crimson dark; Blue and Green take Sajha's palettes, the blue accent one step darker for text contrast): a solid navigation gradient (crimson-deep → crimson → indigo) with white text and icons in the top bar, and deep-crimson page headings.
- **Changed:** the top bar's separate live dot is gone: the **Live** button carries this page's stream too (glowing while ticks arrive, an amber ring while it reconnects, steady without a stream; red while paused), and its tooltip says both.
- **Changed:** **Forms** is an entry of the **Views** menu (the forms home, `/forms`), not a top-bar menu listing every pack (that list ran off the screen); Views is lit on any form page. A test checks that a top-bar menu stays open until the person closes it.
- **Added: maps with regions (Rachana `map` panel, BI map drill).** A new Sutra panel kind, `map` (the 22nd): the world with a country highlighted (`highlight: $.iso3`), a click on any country opening that country's entity (`link: { kind: jurisdiction, id: "JUR-{iso3}" }`; a country with no entity is not clickable and says so on hover), optional `shade` (a field of every entity of a kind, with a legend: GDP per head from the economics pack) and `region` (one country's regions instead of the world); small territories are clickable dots; the places a click opens are also listed as links (keyboard, screen reader, phone).
