- **Fixed:** **an embedded `<drishti-view>` no longer repaints when the stream's first look is a generation ahead of the page.** The element disposed and re-booted every enhancer one to three times within 100 ms of going live (resetting scroll, selection and focus, and flickering for the host). It now fetches the view again and applies only the differences as patches in place (also after a reconnect); a repaint (new `drishti:paint` event) happens only for a real change. Test: ten seconds of ticks, one paint per element, zoom/focus/scroll kept, three browsers.
- **Added:** **schema-driven Sutra generation** ([docs/guides/SCHEMA_TO_PACK.md](docs/guides/SCHEMA_TO_PACK.md)). One or many JSON Schema files (draft 2020-12 and 07; `$ref`/`$defs`/`definitions`, `allOf`, `oneOf`/`anyOf` with or without a `discriminator`, nested objects, arrays of objects, enums, formats, `examples`, `required`, titles and descriptions, JSON or YAML) become a pack: the **kind** (from `x-drishti-kind`, a short title, the `$id` or the file name), the **key** (`x-drishti-role: key`, else the required id-like field, asking when it is ambiguous), **links** between the kinds (`x-drishti-link`, `*Id` fields that name another kind of the batch, or values that are another kind's keys), **labels and panel titles** from `title`, **formats** from `type`/`format`/`x-drishti-format` and a **strip** of the most important numbers (required and described first), a **Sutra per `oneOf` variant** (or per value of a chosen match column, with `match.where`), **About-this-page text** from the descriptions (`config/about.yaml`), enum fields as status fields and as **dropdown control suggestions** (a hook, ready for the controls feature), and **tests** from the schema's own `examples` or synthetic samples generated from it (named `sample-synthetic-N.json`, marked synthetic everywhere). Real JSON Lines documents win over synthetic ones; the schemas add labels, descriptions and links. The rules and every `x-drishti-*` annotation are in the new guide; the code is `drishti-console/core/schemakit/` (pure Python, shared by the console and the command line).
- **Added:** `drishti.py sutra design --schema FILE...` (the Sutras the schemas describe, printed or written under `--out` with their tests) and `drishti.py pack make --schema FILE|DIR... [DATA_FOLDER]` (alone or with a folder of JSON Lines; the same output folder, bundle, `README.txt` and `MANIFEST.json` as before, with many kinds). New flags: `--match none`, `--mnemonic`, `--strip`, `--count`, `--code`, `--description`, `--connector`, `--yes`. `pack make` without `--schema` is unchanged.
- **Added:** **Build → New pack** (`/build/pack/new`, a Build menu entry; the author power builds and downloads, an administrator deploys): five steps. 1 **Sources**: choose or drop many schema files and/or a folder of JSON / JSON Lines; the browser streams and samples them (reservoir, deterministic; file count, rows, size and per-file progress shown, caps configurable) and sends only the sample and the counts. 2 **Kinds**: one card per kind with the detected key, links (and a graph of the kinds), match column (with the number of Sutras it creates), business date and mnemonic, all editable; a kind another pack already defines is flagged. 3 **Pack**: name, code, title, version, connector names and templates per kind (a pack refers to its sources by logical name only), mnemonic conflicts. 4 **Preview**: every Sutra drafted by the server as a background job (progress, cancel) and rendered on its samples (real, example or synthetic, labelled), with its YAML, the About text it carries, its samples and a panel-by-sample check; any Sutra opens in the workbench. 5 **Output**: the bundle in the format of `pack bundle` (checksum, manifest), or **Deploy**: handed to Admin → Packs → Deploy archive, whose checks and preview come first and whose rollback is unchanged. The wizard's state is kept as a draft (a design named `New pack: <name>`) and resumed. New settings `builder.pack_*` and `builder.tools_dir`.
- **Added:** `GET /api/v1/studio/settings` also answers `author` (the caller's role has the author power, whether or not saving is on). Admin → Packs accepts `?bundle=<job>` to take a bundle from New pack.
- **Added:** **TLS for connectors, one set of settings, and Kafka with every security option.** A shared module in the plugin API (`com.ash.drishti.api.tls`: `TlsSettings`, `TlsContexts`, `TlsMaterial`, `Secrets`; pure JDK) reads `tls.*` settings (`enabled`, `ca-file`, `truststore`, `trust-jvm-default`, `cert-file`, `key-file`, `keystore`, `key-alias`, `protocols`, `cipher-suites`, `verify-hostname`, `insecure-trust-all`) and builds the `SSLContext`, key and trust managers and `SSLParameters`: trust from a PEM bundle, a PKCS12 or JKS truststore, the JVM default, or any merge; a client identity (mutual TLS) from PEM files (PKCS#8, PKCS#1 RSA, SEC1 EC, encrypted PKCS#8) or a keystore with an alias; passwords as `${ENV}` placeholders or `-file` paths; hostname verification on, `verify-hostname: false` warns at every start; `insecure-trust-all` refused unless `DRISHTI_ALLOW_INSECURE_TLS=true`. A wrong file, password or key/certificate pair stops the connector at start naming the setting, the file and the reason; a certificate within 30 days of expiry (or expired) is warned about and adds a sentence to the source's health text (Admin → Health). Reference: [docs/connectors/TLS.md](docs/connectors/TLS.md) (every setting, openssl/keytool conversions, rotation, troubleshooting); for plugin authors: [CONNECTOR_DEVELOPER_GUIDE](docs/connectors/CONNECTOR_DEVELOPER_GUIDE.md#tls-for-a-connector-the-shared-module). The other connectors will take the module on in a later change; `drishti-testkit` gains `TestPki` (certificates generated at test time).
- **Added:** **Kafka: `security.protocol` (PLAINTEXT, SSL, SASL_PLAINTEXT, SASL_SSL), `tls.*`, `sasl.*` (PLAIN, SCRAM-SHA-256/512, OAUTHBEARER client credentials, GSSAPI/Kerberos), `flavour: confluent` and Schema Registry.** First-class settings translate to Kafka client properties (PEM becomes `ssl.keystore.type=PEM` / `ssl.truststore.type=PEM`; stores are handed over as they are); `client.<property>` is still passed through and wins. The Confluent preset sets `SASL_SSL`, `PLAIN` with `api-key` / `api-secret`, `client.dns.lookup=use_all_dns_ips` and tuned timeouts. With `schema-registry.url` (basic or bearer auth, TLS through `schema-registry.tls.*`) the connector strips the Confluent wire format (zero byte and 4-byte schema id) and reads JSON Schema payloads as they are and Avro payloads decoded with the writer schema fetched by id (Apache Avro 1.12, Apache-2.0, added to the Kafka plugin) as JSON; Protobuf is not supported yet; an unreadable message is skipped and logged, never read as a delete. Tested against Apache Kafka 3.9 in Docker (mutual TLS in PEM and PKCS12/JKS forms, SASL_SSL SCRAM-SHA-512, refusals) plus unit tests of every property combination and of the wire format with recorded bytes. [KAFKA_CONNECTOR.md › Security: TLS, SASL and Confluent](docs/connectors/KAFKA_CONNECTOR.md#11-security-tls-sasl-and-confluent).
- **Added:** **ActiveMQ over `ssl://` and RabbitMQ over `amqps://` with the shared `tls.*` settings**, no JVM-wide `-Djavax.net.ssl.*` properties needed: a private CA, truststores, a client certificate (PEM or keystore), hostname verification. RabbitMQ also takes `auth-mechanism: external` to log in with the client certificate (SASL EXTERNAL). The whole message-source contract runs over TLS in the tests against ActiveMQ Classic 6.1.7 and RabbitMQ 4.1 in Docker, with a client certificate required. [ACTIVEMQ_CONNECTOR.md › Security](docs/connectors/ACTIVEMQ_CONNECTOR.md#14-security), [RABBITMQ_CONNECTOR.md › Security](docs/connectors/RABBITMQ_CONNECTOR.md#12-security).
- **Added:** **connector files** ([CONNECTOR_FILES.md](docs/connectors/CONNECTOR_FILES.md)). A connector is now a site resource: one YAML file per connector in `config/connectors/<name>.yaml`, the file name being the connector's name (`plugin`, `enabled`, `kinds`, `description`, `settings`; `${VAR:default}` and `file:` references, secrets never written in clear). Settings `drishti.sources.connectors-dir` (`DRISHTI_CONNECTORS_DIR`), `connectors-watch` (`auto`, `poll`, `off`), `connectors-poll` (5s), `connectors-drain` (3s) and `connectors-generate` (true). Every earlier text of a file is kept in `.history/`.
- **Added:** **live reload.** The folder is watched (file events with a polling safety net); only the connector whose file changed is started, restarted or stopped, and reads in flight get `connectors-drain` to finish. A bad file keeps the last good configuration running and appears in Admin -> Health (`connectors` block: `badFiles`, `watch`, `deprecated`) and in the UI.
- **Changed:** **packs name connectors and suggest templates.** A pack lists the connectors it reads through and routes its kinds to them; its `connectors:` mapping is read as TEMPLATES. At the first start the server writes a file from a template when none exists (never overwrites); a file replaces a template wholesale. A route to a connector with no file loads the pack and says so (`DRS-1011` on a read; pack rows in Health show `connectorsNotConfigured`; the deploy preview lists missing connectors).
- **Added:** **Admin -> Connectors** (`/admin/connectors`): list, a form generated from the plugin's settings, a YAML tab, test connection, save with versions, enable and disable, reset to the pack's template, delete, history with diff and restore. Scope `packs:admin` also covers writes to `/api/v1/admin/connectors/**`; audit actions `connector-saved`, `-deleted`, `-reset`, `-enabled`, `-disabled`, `-restored`, `-tested`, `-generated`, `-migrated`.
- **Added:** **connector API and CLI.** `/api/v1/admin/connectors` (list, plugins, get, put with ETag, delete, reset, enable, test, history, restore) and `drishti.py connector list|get|apply|delete|test|plugins|enable|disable|reset|history`.
- **Changed:** the Admin -> Packs **Data source** panel is a read-only view of the pack's connectors, with links to Admin -> Connectors; the `PUT`, `DELETE` and `test` calls of `/admin/packs/{name}/datasource` and `drishti.py server packs datasource set|test|reset` are gone.
- **Changed (migration):** the old per-pack data-source overrides (`data/packs/settings/<pack>.yaml`, `drishti.packs.settings-dir`, `DRISHTI_PACKS_SETTINGS`) are migrated once at start into connector files; the originals are kept in `data/packs/settings/.migrated/`; an override that cannot be placed is left in place and reported.
- **Deprecated:** `drishti.sources.connectors` in `application.yaml` (and in profiles and `DRISHTI_SOURCES_CONNECTORS_*` variables) and the per-store profiles (`postgres`, `duckdb`, `aerospike`, `mongodb`, `iceberg`, `redis`, `files`) still work, with a start-up warning that names the equivalent file; examples for every store are in `config/connectors.examples/`.
- **Added:** error codes `DRS-1011` (a read routed to a connector that has no file), `DRS-5031`, `DRS-5032` and `DRS-5033` (connector file and API errors; see TROUBLESHOOTING).
- **Added:** plugin `settingSpecs()` in the `SourcePlugin` SPI: a plugin declares the settings it reads for the connector form and validator (the server falls back to its catalogue for the shipped plugins).
- **Fixed:** an embedded view that repaints (a refresh, when the stream's first look is newer than the page fetched) **keeps a zoomed panel zoomed** (and its focus), and disposing its charts no longer raises Trusted Types violations on a strict host (ECharts' SVG painter emptied its elements with `innerHTML = ''`). The zoom browser tests click again if a click straddled the swap.
- **Added:** **Rūpaka phase 0 proof of concept, off by default** (`drishti.bi.poc.enabled`, console `bi.poc_enabled`; `docs/architecture/RUPAKA_POC.md`). Throw-away code that tests the risky parts of the BI design: `POST /api/v1/bi/poc/query` (a masked aggregation from embedded DuckDB over a generated Parquet lake, answered as Apache Arrow IPC), `GET /api/v1/bi/poc/rows` (live rows for Perspective, on the console's one live channel), `GET /api/v1/bi/poc/bench` (JSON column vs typed columns vs rollup); the console page `/bi/poc` (top-bar **BI** entry and the reserved command `RUPAKA <GO>`) with Perspective (vendored, 3.9 MB), DuckDB-Wasm (installed by `tools/fetch-duckdb-wasm.sh`, 36 MB, not committed) and a masks-proven panel; `'wasm-unsafe-eval'` and Perspective's style hashes on that one page only.
- **Added:** **Sign in and Sign out are easy to find.** With sign-in on, the landing page's navigation shows **Sign in** (to `/login?next=<this page>`, the target checked as a path on this console) to anyone not signed in, and your name with a small menu (Open the terminal, My account, Sign out) once signed in. With sign-in off, the top bar's user menu says **Sign-in is off on this server** with a **How to turn it on** link (USER_MANAGEMENT, "Signing in and out"), and `/login` says so and offers the terminal instead of a form that cannot work. `/login` links back to the home page; **Sign out** lands on the landing page with a "Signed out" notice. Tests: `test_sign_links.py`, `test_sign_links_browser.py`.
- **Changed:** a zoomed panel's table **fits the height**: the pager gets a temporary *Fit (n)* page size (panel height over the real row height, at least 5) that keeps the top row on screen, follows window resizes, tab switches and live row changes, yields to an explicit choice until restore, and is never saved; restore puts back the previous size and page. Also inside `<drishti-view>`.
- **Added:** **zoom a panel.** Every panel's heading has a zoom button (`aria-pressed`, *Expand/Restore <title>*) and `Z` on a focused panel expands it over the view area, under the top bar and above the function keys, with page scroll locked, the other panels inert, live patches still applied and charts redrawn at the new size; `Esc` (after any open drawer or popover) or the button restores it and returns the focus to the button. The state is the URL hash `#zoom=<panel>` (reload or share; an unknown id is ignored), not your saved layout; layout mode and zoom exclude each other; `Shift+Z` asks for true full screen; on a phone the button is 44 pixels and the zoomed panel fills the screen; print shows the zoomed panel alone. Inside `<drishti-view>` the panel fills the element's own box (`static/js/zoom.js`, a root-scoped enhancer). USER_GUIDE "Zoom a panel", pictures by `tools/docs/screenshots.py --guide zoom`.
- **Changed:** the favicon and app icons are the logo mark (braces and eye) filling the icon, generated in every size by `tools/make_icons.py`; `/favicon.ico` is served.
- **Added:** **the full `<drishti-view>`** (Drishti Elements step 9). The library is now ES modules under `web/embed/` (served as `/embed/v1/m/<name>.js`, one `<script type="module">` line still loads it). Server markup goes through an allow-list sanitiser (no scripts, iframes, `on*`, inline styles or `javascript:` links reach the shadow root) and one Trusted Types policy, `drishti-elements`, so a host with `require-trusted-types-for 'script'; trusted-types drishti-elements` works. New: `loading`/`empty`/`error` slots for host content, `density` and a reserved `panel` attribute, the `drishti:token-needed` event, a token provider set late reloads elements that failed with a 401, and tokens are renewed early enough for short lifetimes (a 30 s token at 18 s). The About drawer's body is written through `options.setHtml` (the console's own pages are unchanged). Browser tests (Chromium, Firefox, WebKit) cover the sanitiser, Trusted Types, live token renewal past the first token's expiry and slots.
- **Added:** **Admin → Embedding** (Drishti Elements step 10): `/admin/embedding` lists the host applications with origins, sign-in method, scopes and kinds, status and usage (tokens issued, calls, live streams, refusals by code, last use); *New application* shows the client secret once, *Rotate secret* shows the new one once, *Disable* and *Enable* stop and resume an application without deleting it, *Delete* confirms first. Applications can be declared in configuration (`drishti.embed.apps`, GitOps: only the secret's SHA-256 is written) and are read-only, marked *from config*. The server counts usage in memory (`GET /api/v1/admin/embed/usage`, Micrometer `drishti.embed.*` by `app`), and has `POST /api/v1/admin/embed/apps/{id}/disable` and `/enable`.
- **Added:** **embedded views, the console's embed API** (Drishti Elements step 7). The proof of concept's `/embed/v1/poc/*` routes are now the versioned API under `/embed/v1` (views, About at `/views/{kind}/{id}/about`, pivot rows at `/views/{kind}/{id}/panels/{panel}/records`, `/resolve`, the live `/channel`; the `poc` paths are gone). `EmbedAuth` verifies the server's ES256 embed token in the console (key set from `/api/v1/embed/jwks`, cached, one throttled refetch on an unknown key id; expiry, audience, the token's own origins) before the token is passed on to the server, which still decides. A live stream outlives its token: at expiry the console sends `event: token`, the element posts a fresh token to `POST /embed/v1/channel/{cid}/token` and nothing repaints; with none within `embed.token_grace_seconds` the stream ends `DRS-8001`; the server is re-asked every `embed.recheck_seconds`. New console settings `embed.rate_per_minute` (`429 DRS-8004` with `Retry-After`), `embed.max_streams`, `embed.jwks_ttl_seconds`, `embed.jwks_min_refetch_seconds`, `embed.audiences`, `embed.recheck_seconds`, `embed.token_grace_seconds`, `embed.compress_min_bytes`; a `Drishti-Embed-Api` major other than 1 is `410 DRS-8006`; payloads are gzip or Brotli compressed. The console now needs the `cryptography` package (`brotli` optional).
- **Changed:** the console listens on every interface (`0.0.0.0`) by default, so it opens from other machines and phones; `DRISHTI_CONSOLE_HOST=127.0.0.1` keeps it to this machine.
- **Changed (mobile friendly):** `<drishti-view>` answers to its own width, not the host page's viewport: the element is a size container and the generated sheet rewrites the console's width `@media` rules to `@container drishti (...)` (non-width media are kept; `test_element_sheet.py` fails if a width `@media` is left). In a 360 px sidebar of a desktop page a view takes the narrow layout; on a phone it has no sideways scroll, panels stack, controls are at least 44 px, hints open by tap, tables scroll inside their panel and the About drawer is a bottom sheet when the element is narrow (tests in Chromium, Firefox and WebKit). The sample host page is responsive too. The console's page-width sweep (390, 1600, 2560 px) now includes the data loads pages, collaboration admin, the inbox, the no-access share page and the landing page's way back. Documented in `ELEMENTS.md` and the developer guide.
- **Changed (touch):** on touch devices (`pointer: coarse`) every control the console shows is at least 44 px in both directions (top bar, function keys, table pagers and filters, inputs, buttons, link lists, help contents), from a new last-loaded `css/touch.css` (also in the element sheet); desktop sizes are unchanged. Code examples in the help scroll sideways inside their box instead of being cut off. `test_page_widths_browser.py` gained a 390 px touch sweep over all pages against the real server (skipped without the built jar): every page shows content rather than a problem page, no tap target is under 44 px (running-text links excepted), no text is clipped.
- **Added:** **embedded views, server side** (Drishti Elements steps 1 to 3; off by default, `drishti.embed.enabled`). Host applications are registered by an administrator (`/api/v1/admin/embed/apps`: exact origins, a client secret shown once and/or public keys, kinds, scopes, token lifetime, rates; audited) and their backends buy short embed tokens by the RFC 8693 token exchange (`POST /api/v1/embed/token`: client secret or `private_key_jwt`, and the user's OIDC ID token or a JWT the host signs about the user; there is no plain-username path). The token is an ES256 JWT of the server's own key (`/api/v1/embed/jwks`), audience-bound, 5 minutes, read-only (`embed:view`, `embed:about`). Every embed call re-checks the token, the host application, the user, the `Origin`, the scope, the kind and the rates, and **always masks** whatever the user's roles say; the access log says `embed:<app>`. `provenance.masked` and `provenance.maskedPanels` count what a caller's masks hid in every ViewModel and live frame. New codes `DRS-8001` to `DRS-8006`. The console's dev-only token path of the proof of concept is gone: `tools/elements-demo` now signs a user assertion and exchanges it at the server.
- **Changed (Elements build steps 4, 5 and 8, no change to the console's behaviour):** the live channel is a class, `core/channel.py` `ChannelSession`, shared by `/api/channel` and the embed stream (identity passed in, owner = user plus host application); the view's title, strip, panels and provenance banners are macros (`templates/_macros/view.html`) used by the full page and the embed payload, so both render a panel with the same template; `<drishti-view>`'s stylesheet is generated by `python3 tools/elements_sheet.py` into `drishti-console/web/elements/` (versioned by content hash, only the Bootstrap Icons glyphs the macros use: 80.2 KB, 15.7 KB gzipped, down from 30.5 KB) and a test fails when it is stale or a macro uses a glyph it lacks.
- **Changed:** the console's view scripts (`tables`, `tree-rows`, `pivot`, `view`, `charts`, `about`, `about-hints`) are root-scoped modules: each exposes `init(root, options)` (root = the document or a ShadowRoot) and the console calls `init(document)`, so it behaves as before. `<drishti-view>` now uses the same copies for sorting and paging, tree rows, pivots (no saving), charts, panel `?` popovers and the About drawer; keys are bound to the element, not the host page (Elements, step 6).
- **Added (proof of concept, off by default):** `<drishti-view>`, a live Drishti view inside another web application's page in a Shadow DOM, no iframe: the console's `/embed/v1/` (view as per-panel HTML, one channel, resolve, the element's script and sheet; `embed.poc.*`, exact-origin CORS, always masked) and a sample host application on its own origin (`tools/elements-demo/`). Acceptance runs in Chromium, Firefox and WebKit; results in `docs/architecture/ELEMENTS.md`, step 0. The token path is a dev-only stand-in for the planned RFC 8693 exchange.
- **Added:** Admin menu → **Data loads** (`/admin/loads`): every loaded pack at a glance (today's expectations, latest loads, what needs attention); the Admin menu also lists **Collaboration**. Both were reachable only from tabs or a row button before.
- **Docs:** design for **Drishti Elements** (`docs/architecture/ELEMENTS.md`, proposed): live Drishti views inside other web applications without iframes, as a `<drishti-view>` Web Component fed by HTML over the wire from the console, with embed tokens by token exchange (RFC 8693), the decisions and a build plan.

## 1.18.0 — Data loads: the batch-landed signal, expectations and late data (2026-10-06)
- **Added:** **data loads**, the "batch landed" signal. Drishti does not ingest (that stays with your ETL or DishtaYantra); what only it can do is act on new data. `POST /api/v1/packs/{pack}/loads {kind, businessDate|asOf, status ready|failed, rows, rejected, source, batchId, note}` (idempotent per pack, kind, date and batch; a later `failed` or a re-load is recorded as such; an administrator, or a personal token with the new least-privilege scope `loads:write` whose user's roles open the kind; `DRS-5011` unknown pack, `DRS-5012` unknown kind) records the load, drops the caches of the connectors that serve the kind, verifies the kind has entities on the date (`verified` or `not-found`), evaluates the alert rules on the kind for that business date, optionally opens N sample entities, and tells the people the pack names (inbox, bell, and an email through the outbox when asked), each as a step with its own result. **Expectations** per pack and kind (`loads:` in `pack.yaml` plus an administrator's override file, like the data source: due time, zone, business-day calendar) flag late and missing loads once (a notice, and `data late: my-bank/trade 2026-10-06` on Admin → Health, which turns DEGRADED) and clear when the load lands. New Admin → Packs → **Data loads** page per pack (history with step results, expectations with today's state, filters, settings; keyboard, phone, themes), `drishti.py data landed` (exit 1 when not verified) and `data loads`, settings `drishti.loads.*`, audit rows `data-load-*`. New guide [DATA_LOADS.md](docs/guides/DATA_LOADS.md) (concepts, API, CLI, configuration, Airflow, cron and DishtaYantra recipes, troubleshooting, FAQ); OPERATIONALISING, DEMO_DATA and CLI_GUIDE now say plainly that `data ingest`, `--watch` and `pack make` are for staging, small setups and proofs of concept, not production ETL.

## 1.17.1 — The logo leads home and back, hot reload by polling, a nine-minute drill (2026-10-06)
- **Fixed:** Sutra hot reload no longer stops when the operating system has no file watches left (Linux *User limit of inotify watches reached*): it polls the Sutra files instead (`drishti.rachana.watch: auto|poll`, `poll-interval`); Health shows `hotReload: POLLING`. Before, edits were ignored until a restart.
- **Changed:** the drill is about 2.5 times faster with the same tests: Maven test classes run in parallel forks (`drishti.test.forks`), the console suite on pytest-xdist workers, the Java 25 verify (in a copy of the committed tree) beside the console and tool tests; `tools/drill.sh --docs` (chosen by itself when only documentation changed) is the light drill and `--no-push` verifies without pushing; a browser wait that timed out on `net::ERR_NETWORK_CHANGED` reloads the page once. Tests that depended on the order they ran in were fixed (the stand-in server's state is restored per test, `PackDeployApiTest` restores the pack folders it changed, a bridge test ignores the JDK's own HTTP server log). See the developer guide, section 2.6.
- **Changed:** the name and logo in the top bar lead to the landing page from every page, signed in or not; a signed-in visitor gets **← Back to …** (Alt+B) to the page they came from (same-site paths only).

## 1.17.0 — JSON Lines to a deployed pack: the drishti.py command line, pack make, admin deploy (2026-10-05)
- **Added:** Admin → Packs **Deploy an archive**, **History and roll back**, and per-pack **Data source**. An archive (the pack only, never data; the `.tar.gz` of `pack bundle`, or a `.zip`) is uploaded as the raw body (`POST /api/v1/admin/packs/deploy`, 413 above `drishti.packs.deploy.max-archive-mb`), checked by the server (SHA-256, optional Ed25519 signature by a `registry.trusted-keys` publisher, tar/zip slip, links, size and file-count limits, `MANIFEST.json` checksums as `pack verify`, required server version, Sutra lint and tests by the same checker, parent packs) and previewed against the running version with the same four levels as `pack diff` (breaking, selection, layout, change; the Java and Python rule sets share test cases). Confirming swaps it into `drishti.packs.installed-dir`, keeps the replaced version (`keep-versions`, 5), restarts in place with the existing automatic undo, and records the deployment (`deploy-history.jsonl`, audit rows); `POST /admin/packs/{name}/rollback` goes back to a kept version or to the shipped copy. The data source of a pack (view, edit, **Test connection** with business dates and row counts per kind, reset) is saved to `data/packs/settings/<pack>.yaml`, never into the pack, so redeploys keep it; precedence environment > override file > pack; credentials only as `${ENV}` references; shown in the console as pack default / overridden / set by the site. Token scope `packs:admin` covers all of it. CLI: `drishti.py server packs deploy|history|rollback|datasource get|set|test|reset`. Docs: OPERATIONALISING section 17, PACKS, CLI_GUIDE, API_GUIDE, CONFIGURATION.
- **Added:** `GET /api/v1/me/token` (the calling token's id, scopes and expiry; 404 for a session), used by `drishti.py doctor`; `SourcePlugin.inventory` (dates and row counts, implemented by the Delta and file connectors); `DRISHTI_DOCTOR_PORTS` to check other ports than 18480/17480.
- **Fixed:** the explain API's match trace now includes the chosen Sutra (`candidates[].chosen`), so `view explain` shows the whole trace exactly; the CLI_GUIDE captures of `server smoke`, `doctor` (no false red "jar missing") and `view get|explain` were retaken.
- **Added:** pack authoring tools in `drishti.py`: `data profile` (streaming, bounded memory: every field with types, coverage, distinct count and examples, candidate key, date and match columns with an explained score, and a ready `pack make` line), `pack regenerate PACK INPUTS` (generates again and three-way merges into the pack against a baseline that `pack new`/`pack make` now store in `<pack>/.generated/`: new groups added, untouched files refreshed, your edits kept, edits on both sides merged line by line or marked as conflicts and listed, exit 1; `--dry-run`; nothing of yours is overwritten), `pack diff OLD NEW` (folders or bundles: Sutras added/removed/changed, kinds and mnemonics removed or renamed flagged BREAKING, match where/priority flagged "screen selection changes", about, glossary and data layout changes; `--unified`, `--fail-on breaking|any|none`), `pack catalogue` (Markdown or one HTML page for business review: kinds, glossary, every Sutra with match rule and panels, optional `sutra preview` snapshots) and `pack i18n export|import` (About text through a CSV into `config/about.<lang>.yaml`, reporting missing and extra keys). Bundles leave `.generated/` out. Tests `tools/test_pack_authoring.py` (in the drill); CLI_GUIDE sections with real output; PyCharm `.run/` samples.
- **Added:** five CLI commands. `drishti.py server smoke` (health, packs loaded and enabled, no Sutra problems, then sample views of every kind on the latest and an earlier business date with timings; exit 1 on any failure), `doctor` (green/yellow/red checks of Java 21+, the server jar, Python and optional libraries, directories, ports 18480/17480 and, with `--server`, the token and its scopes, each with a fix), `data ingest --watch DIR` (a drop folder: stable-size check, each file once through a state file written after success, `--done-dir`, `--once` for cron, `--interval`), `view get|explain KIND/ID [--date D]` (the rendered view and the About answer as text tables, with the caller's masks) and `completion bash|zsh|powershell` (generated from the command tree). CLI_GUIDE has a section for each with real output; `.run/` has PyCharm configurations for smoke, doctor and view get.
- **Added:** `drishti.py pack make INPUT --kind K --match COL --name N`: one command from a folder (or file) of JSON Lines to ONE folder to deploy: `pack/<name>/` (Sutras, tests, about), the data in `data/delta` (or `data/files`) partitioned by business date, a checksummed `bundle/`, `config/drishti-site.yaml`, `run-server.sh`/`.ps1`, `MANIFEST.json` and a plain-text `README.txt` (deploy, verify, lifting the data from Delta, new days, rollback, troubleshooting) with the real kind, ids and dates filled in. It detects `--key` (present and unique in every document) and `--date` (a date field in at least 95% of the documents) when not given, says why, then runs `pack check` and `pack verify`. `pack new` gained `--version`. CLI_GUIDE has a "Quickest path" section and a much fuller PyCharm section (interpreter, a run configuration per task, debugging, sharing through `.run/`, with sample `.run/*.run.xml` files).
- **Fixed:** `sutra lint` and `sutra test` (and `drishti.py pack check`) load the packs their command line names, so a pack is checked with its own About text and glossary whatever `DRISHTI_PACKS` says; a pack outside `packs/` is read from its parent folder. Before, market-risk and the other banking packs showed false help-coverage failures from the command line.
- **Added:** personal API tokens (`drk_…`) may carry write scopes so tools, CI and GitOps can act: `design:write`, `design:approve`, `packs:admin` (config `drishti.security.token-scopes`; no `admin` or `data:admin` scope, and users, roles, tokens, sign-in and personal or collaboration state stay closed via `token-never`). Chosen on My account → API tokens (or `POST /me/tokens {scopes}`), stored with the token, enforced in one place (`TokenScopes` in the token filter) as scope **and** the user's current roles; a write token must expire (`token-write-max-days`, 90); every write is audited as `token-write` (token id, never the secret) and each refusal as `token-denied`. Existing tokens keep reading only. `TokenScopeGuardTest` fails when a non-GET endpoint declares no scope. CLI_GUIDE, API_GUIDE, CLIENTS, USER_MANAGEMENT and CONFIGURATION updated.
- **Added:** `tools/drishti.py`, one command line (stdlib + PyYAML) for `sutra shape|design|gen|lint|test|preview`, `pack new|check|about-check|publish|keygen|verify|install`, `data ingest|load`, `server health|packs list|load|unload|on|off`, `design list|create|get|save|check|propose|approve|reject|export|import|bind` (REST, token from `DRISHTI_TOKEN` or `--token-file`) and `docs shots`, with `--json`, exit codes 0/1/2 and `pack new --load --server`; the generated `pack.yaml` `ingest:` block is documented as an optional tooling key the server ignores; new guide `docs/guides/CLI_GUIDE.md` (every command with real output, recipes, running it from PyCharm).

- **Added:** the offline path to production: `drishti.py pack bundle` (a reproducible `<name>-<version>.tar.gz` with `.sha256` and a MANIFEST of checksums, kinds, required server version and data layout), `pack verify` (archive and file checksums, schema, server version, `sutra lint`/`test`; also checks a deployed folder for drift), `pack deploy` (atomic copy into a packs folder, previous version kept in a backup folder, `--dry-run`, never touches a running server) and `pack rollback`, with `tools/packbundle.py`, `tools/test_pack_bundle.py` and the guide docs/guides/OPERATIONALISING.md (artifacts and where each goes, promotion by copying the same bundle, data, CI, permissions, Docker/Kubernetes, Windows, troubleshooting). `pack verify --registry` is the old registry check.- **Added:** `tools/drishti.py`, one command line (stdlib + PyYAML) for `sutra shape|design|gen|lint|test|preview`, `pack new|check|about-check|publish|keygen|verify|install`, `data ingest|load`, `server health|packs list|load|unload|on|off`, `design list|create|get|save|check|propose|approve|reject|export|import|bind` (REST, token from `DRISHTI_TOKEN` or `--token-file`) and `docs shots`, with `--json`, exit codes 0/1/2 and `pack new --load --server`; the generated `pack.yaml` `ingest:` block is documented as an optional tooling key the server ignores; new guide `docs/guides/CLI_GUIDE.md` (every command with real output, recipes, running it from PyCharm).
- **Added:** `sutra design --each <dir>` (one draft per subfolder of samples in one JVM), `tools/sutragen.py` (Sutras from JSONL, one per match-field value, `--date`, samples across dates), `tools/packgen/pack_from_jsonl.py` (a complete dated pack from JSONL, linted and tested) and `tools/ingest_jsonl.py` (JSONL into a Delta lake or the File connector's files, idempotent per business date); inputs are folders or single files. See PACK_DEVELOPER_GUIDE "Generating a pack from JSON Lines".
- **Changed:** the console module is `drishti-console/` (was `console/`), named like the other modules. Recreate its virtual environment once: `rm -rf drishti-console/.venv` and follow QUICKSTART step 3; point IDE run configurations at `drishti-console/run_drishti_web.py` and `drishti-console/.venv`.

## 1.16.0 — Java 21 for production, About this page, Share and Discussion (2026-10-05)

- **Collaboration behaviour pass (B3):** Admin > Collaboration gets a mail outbox view (tries, last error, dead letters, send again, test mail), bridge rows show tries and last error, and a share that asks for email while the mail server is failing says so; revision history, comments and shares use the person's Clock time zone (pictures use the business zone); error lines print the code once and `Retry-After` reaches the browser; the bridge Test waits for the bridge (`collab.bridge_test_timeout_seconds`) and names the right setting; the Edit button goes when the window closes and the message says "the 15-minute edit window"; the Discussion box fits a 390 px phone; a lone email notice goes after 10 s (`email.coalesce-window`) instead of 2 minutes; the compliance export streams through the console; `GET /admin/collab/threads/hidden` lists hidden comments for the admin page.

- **Data safety QA fixes (2026-10-05):** no oracle for masked values (an author without `raw` gets no warning or refusal, reads their own text as written, each hit is audited as `collab.masked-copy`); copies are matched case-insensitively after NFKC, spacing, zero-width and look-alike folding; emails and chat posts carry values rendered per recipient (quotes filled in share emails and bridges too; `link-only` unchanged); share pictures freeze their rights profile at share time and can only narrow later; tamper evidence covers the live comment row, the thread head, holds and the audit trail (`drishti_collab_seal`), and `verify` reports unreadable rows instead of failing; tokens of deleted or disabled users are refused (`drishti.security.registered-users-only`); email templates drop their licence comment; `/collab` reports real email availability; names lose control characters.
- **Watermarked snapshots (collaboration step 10):** off by default (`drishti.collab.snapshots.enabled`, per pack `packs.<pack>.snapshots.enabled`); a share may include a server-drawn PNG (Java2D, headless) of the view or panel, made for the most restrictive recipient (masks of anyone without `raw`, panels any recipient may not open left out), watermarked with sender, recipients, time, data date, generation and link; limits (`snapshots.width|max-height|max-panels|max-rows|max-bytes|timeout`), a cache, `DRS-7015`/`DRS-7016`, an access-log `export` row per picture, `POST /shares/preview-picture`, `GET /shares/{id}/picture`, an email attachment under `email.content: comment`, and the share dialog's *Include a picture* with a preview.
- **Collaboration server polish:** the file inbox and outbox never reuse a sequence number (a high-water mark is kept before rows are purged); bridge posts also write an access-log row; several notices for one person inside `drishti.collab.email.coalesce-window` (default 2m, `0` = off) go out as one digest email; the compliance export adds `inbox.ndjson` and `outbox.ndjson` (no message bodies); `drishti.collab.share.post-to-thread` is the server default when a share request does not say.
- **Collaboration console polish:** the share dialog's tick *Also start a discussion on the view* (default from `drishti.collab.share.post-to-thread`), a reply box on the shared view's banner for the share's sender and recipients, **Admin > Collaboration** (threads and moderation, legal holds, compliance export, chain verify, retention dry run, bridges, role-gated as the API), every collaboration time in the top bar's zone with its label, and quoted values drawn as the page formats the field (`•••` stays for readers without raw). No server change.
- Java 21 is the production runtime: docs, pom enforcer message, Windows script and server Dockerfile (default `eclipse-temurin:21-jre`) no longer say "25 recommended"; Java 25 stays supported (compact object headers there only).
- **Fixed:** in a workspace pane, Alt+0..4 moved the focus again: the view's own date attribute (`data-page-asof`, added with Discussion) no longer looks like the top bar's date form, which made `app.js` stop before its pane keys; `app.js` now looks for the form only.
- **Build:** `mvn package` fails when any runtime dependency needs a JVM newer than Java 21 (`enforceBytecodeVersion`, `maxJdkVersion` 21), so the product always runs on Java 21, the production runtime.
- **Changed (docs):** collaboration documents reconciled to what was built (build step 8): the worked example *Share and Discussion, end to end* ([HOW_IT_FITS.md §3.10](docs/architecture/HOW_IT_FITS.md#310-share-and-discussion-end-to-end)) on MX-20000001 with screenshots (`tools/docs/shots/collab.py`, `docs/guides/img/collab/`), ADR-020, the console guide *Sharing and discussion* (the inbox's help page), moderation and compliance in USER_MANAGEMENT, backups in OPERATIONS, two TROUBLESHOOTING entries, and the as-built differences in COLLABORATION.md (inbox rows are not yet purged by `inbox.keep-days`; `share.post-to-thread` is only reported by `/collab`).
- **Changed (tests):** the drill's Java 21 run skips the container tests (they run on Java 25); the console's browser tests share one live server and console per session instead of one per file; builders run only the tests their change touches, the drill is the one full gate.
- **Changed (drill):** a console test that fails is re-run once; one that then passes is reported as `FLAKY` and logged in `target/drill-flakes.log`, one that fails twice still stops the drill. Browser waits that time out now report the page state, JavaScript errors, failed requests and HTTP errors.
- **Added:** Retention, legal holds and the compliance export ([COLLABORATION.md](docs/architecture/COLLABORATION.md), build step 7, server): `drishti.collab.retention` (`keep-days`, per-kind `kinds`, per-pack `packs.<pack>.retention-days`, default keep forever) with a scheduled purge that removes whole old threads and shares and never what an active legal hold covers; legal holds placed and released by the `compliance` role (scope entity, kind, user, thread or all, with an optional date range, audited); an asynchronous eDiscovery export (JSON Lines in a zip with every revision, the hash-chain proof and a manifest of SHA-256 checksums, streamed in bounded memory, once-only download, audited); `GET /admin/collab/verify` to find a tampered chain; admin search and `/admin/collab/retention/run`; `DRS-7010` for removing a held thread. API only: the admin console page is not built yet. See [OPERATIONS.md](docs/admin/OPERATIONS.md#9a-3-collaboration-retention-legal-holds-and-the-compliance-export).
- **Added:** Ask about this page ([CONTEXT_HELP.md](docs/architecture/CONTEXT_HELP.md), build step 9), optional and **off by default**: `POST /api/v1/views/{kind}/{id}/ask` and a question box at the end of the About drawer, drawn only for packs listed in `drishti.explain.ask.packs`. The server builds the prompt from the caller's own explain answer, its glossary and the pack guide (labels only unless `values: shown`; a masked value is never sent; data and pack text are marked untrusted with a per-request id; no tools), calls an OpenAI-compatible or Anthropic Messages endpoint with a key from `DRISHTI_ASK_KEY`, and limits questions per user (`DRS-4009`), time (`DRS-4008`) and size; questions go to the access log (`ask`), answers are not stored. `DRS-4007` to `DRS-4009` are now live. See [CONFIGURATION.md](docs/admin/CONFIGURATION.md) and the privacy note in [OPERATIONS.md](docs/admin/OPERATIONS.md).
- **Added:** Discussion, the console ([COLLABORATION.md](docs/architecture/COLLABORATION.md), build step 6): **Discussion** (`Alt+N`) is a second tab of the view's side drawer beside About (`?` and `F1` still open About, arrow keys switch tabs; a bottom sheet on a phone). Threads on the whole view, a panel (a comment count beside its `?`) or a field; `@` mentions of people and roles and `{` value quotes (masked values read as •••); reply, edit within the window, retract, resolve, follow and mute, administrator hide and lock; each comment's pin with **Open as it was**; new comments announced live. The old Notes drawer is gone (old notes appear as threads). The account page gains the email opt-outs (share, mention, reply).
- **Fixed (threads):** a note imported from the old Notes is checked for masked values when it is read (it was never checked when written), so a value such as a trader's name typed into an old note shows as ••• to readers without raw; when the entity cannot be read the whole text is hidden from them.
- **Added:** Chat bridges and mail follow-ups ([COLLABORATION.md](docs/architecture/COLLABORATION.md), build step 9): admin-configured bridges (`drishti.collab.bridges`) post a share, comment or mention as "comment + link" to a signed generic webhook, a Microsoft Teams incoming webhook or Workflows, or a Slack incoming webhook, through the outbox (retries, backoff, dead letters), with routing rules per pack, kind and event, URLs and secrets from environment variables only, an allow-list, a per-minute limit, no redirects, never a data value (the note is written as the `render-as` role would read it, quotes read as their paths), audit entries, `GET /admin/collab/bridges` and a test post (`DRS-7013`, `DRS-7014`); also emails for @mentions and replies, panel titles instead of ids in emails, and `inbox.keep-days` now purges old inbox rows.
- **Added:** Email for shares ([COLLABORATION.md](docs/architecture/COLLABORATION.md), build step 4): off until `drishti.collab.email.enabled`, `spring.mail.host` and `drishti.collab.console-url` are set (and sign-in is on). A share writes an outbox row per recipient in its own transaction (`drishti_outbox`, JPA or files, one contract test); a worker claims due rows under a lease, builds the message for the recipient's rights at send time (the kind, id, panel, date, the note with masked values as `•••`, and the link; never a data value; `link-only` per pack with `packs.<pack>.email.content`; text and HTML templates, product name from `drishti.branding.product`, overridable with `email.templates-dir`), sends through `spring-boot-starter-mail`, retries with doubling backoff and makes dead letters, visible and retryable at `GET /api/v1/admin/collab/outbox` and `POST .../outbox/{seq}/retry`; `POST .../mail-test` mails the administrator; `notify.email.{share,mention,reply}` in `/me/settings` opts a person out; metrics `drishti.collab.mail` and `drishti.collab.outbox`. Tests: `OutboxDispatcherTest`, `MailRenderTest`, `ShareMail*Test` against an in-process SMTP server (GreenMail).
- **Added:** Share with a note, the console ([COLLABORATION.md](docs/architecture/COLLABORATION.md), build step 3): **Share** (`Alt+S`) opens a dialog (the view or one panel, the pinned date shown, people and roles by type-ahead from the directory, a note, `Ctrl+Enter` to send, *Copy link* kept) that posts to the step 2 API and reports who was reached and who was not; `/share/{id}` opens the view as it was pinned (date, "known at", generation, panel) with a *Shared by* banner and the note, or a clean no-access page (nothing about the entity; *Switch on <pack>* when the pack is off; a stranger's link looks like no link); the view call carries `X-Drishti-Share`; the bell counts unread notices and alerts (toast on a `notice`, count from `/api/inbox/count`) and opens `/inbox` (`Alt+I`), a page of rows rendered for the reader now with filters, mark read and mark all read; keyboard, phone full-screen sheet, all seven themes; see [USER_GUIDE.md](docs/guides/USER_GUIDE.md#share-a-view-with-a-note).
- **Added:** Comment threads, the server ([COLLABORATION.md](docs/architecture/COLLABORATION.md), build step 5): threads on a view, a panel or a field (`/threads`, `/comments`, `/me/mentions`), visible to whoever may open the entity (kind, pack and a panel's gate kind); every comment is pinned to the business date and generation, edited within `threads.edit-window` then retract-only, hidden by administrators with a reason, and kept as hash-chained revisions; `@user` and `@role` mentions notify only people who may reach the kind; a masked value typed into a comment reads `•••` for readers without `raw`, and `{$.path}` quotes are filled from each reader's own view; share replies (`POST /shares/{id}/replies`) and `postToThread` now work; the old notes were imported as threads and `/notes` is a deprecated facade for one release; stores for files and the identity database, `DRS-7005` to `DRS-7009`.
- **Added:** About this page, step 7 ([CONTEXT_HELP.md](docs/architecture/CONTEXT_HELP.md)): the workbench **About** tab. The About text (the pack's `config/about.yaml`) is a part of a Design, saved as a step of its log (`PUT /builder/designs/{id}/about`; undo, redo, revisions and the stale-revision `409` as for the Sutra), previewed as the About card of the sample being previewed (`GET .../about`, `POST .../about/preview`: the drawer's own code with the Design's text laid over the packs'; `DesignAbout`, `AboutSource`, `AboutPreview`), with the located `DRS-2040`-`2044` problems and the `DRS-2045`-`2047` lint (also in the Problems tab); a *Starter* writes a skeleton for the kind, its panels and the fields without an entry; the pack fragment exports it as `config/about.yaml` (with `about:` in `pack.yaml`) and the import reads it back. Not made live by approval. Tests: `DesignAboutApiTest`, `DesignAboutTest`, `test_build_about.py`, `test_workbench_about_browser.py` (live server, keyboard).
- **Added:** About this page, step 5 ([CONTEXT_HELP.md](docs/architecture/CONTEXT_HELP.md)): real about text for every kind of the QUICKSTART packs (market, counterparty, liquidity, climate and operational risk, retail banking, genomics, politics and economics) and for the trading pack's `trade` and `desk-pnl` (a sentence over the document, a glossary with definitions, units and sign conventions, and about 190 shared product terms), generated by the pack generators from hand-kept sources; 100% of the fields every Sutra shows have a glossary entry, recorded as the floor in each pack's `tests/help-coverage.txt` with new `tests/<sutra>/` fixtures; the translation overlay `about.<lang>.yaml` (key by key over English, strict parse, `?locale=`, the user's new `locale` setting or `Accept-Language`, answer says the language used) with a French demo for the VaR page; pack lint now keys table columns like the drawer and finds a derived field's formula without a provenance (`SourceRouter.describeField`); `AboutPacksTest` and the helpdesk example's `about.yaml` under the strict parser.
- **Added:** Pinned links survive sign-in ([COLLABORATION.md](docs/architecture/COLLABORATION.md) step 1): the sign-in redirect keeps the full path and query (`/login?next=` carries `?asOf=&knownAt=&gen=` and `srv=`), checked same-origin only (`//host`, `/\\host`, backslashes and control characters fall back to `/t`; one `core/nextpath.py` for sign-in, SSO and `/asof`); `?asOf=`/`?knownAt=` are read per request and never saved (a pin without `knownAt` ignores the reader's cookie); *Share* now copies `/v/{kind}/{id}?asOf=&knownAt=&gen=` (the old `/asof?d=…&next=…` form still works); a *Pinned* banner says as it was, latest for the date (`DRS-1007` retried without `knownAt`) or changed since (*What changed*), with *Go live*. Tests: `test_share_link.py`, `test_share_link_browser.py` (sign-in on, `wb_live.live_auth_console`).
- **Added:** Share with a note, the server ([COLLABORATION.md](docs/architecture/COLLABORATION.md), build step 2): `POST /api/v1/shares` sends a note and a pinned link (business date, "known at", generation, source) to users and roles from the directory; each person is checked with the new `Entitlements.mayReach` (role opens the kind and the pack is assigned) and again when they open the share (`GET /api/v1/shares/{id}`: a clean `access: false` for someone who may no longer open it, masked values in the note read `•••` for readers without `raw`, a stranger gets `404 DRS-7001`); `GET /api/v1/directory` is the picker (only users who share a pack with you, roles as groups, rate limited); the inbox (`/me/inbox`, `/count`, `/read`) is rendered when read, with no data value in a notice, and `notice` events ride the existing `/me/alerts/stream` (other servers within `inbox.poll`); the access log gains `share` rows, written in the share's transaction and never dropped (`recordNow`), and `share:sh_…` on views opened through the link (`X-Drishti-Share`); the `collaborate` (default on) and `compliance` role powers; problem codes `DRS-7001` to `DRS-7012`; `drishti.collab.*` keys (rate limits, `text.deny-patterns`, `on-masked-copy`, `directory.scope`, per-pack `share-enabled`); shares and inbox rows live in the identity database or in files (`drishti.collab.store`), and both stores pass one contract test. The console UI is build step 3.
- **Docs:** About this page, step 8 ([CONTEXT_HELP.md](docs/architecture/CONTEXT_HELP.md)): [HOW_IT_FITS.md](docs/architecture/HOW_IT_FITS.md) gets section 3.9 *About this page, end to end* (`?` to `ExplainController`, the view re-run for the caller, `MatchTrace`, emptiness reasons, provenance and health, `about.yaml` through `extends` rendered over the redacted document, the glossary, the drawer; the genomics variant) and `about.yaml` in its picture, pieces and *Where to change what*; the pack developer guide gets *About text and glossary* and a help-desk step (the example pack gets a `config/about.yaml`); the user guide gets *About this page*; the API, Rachana, Sutra, panel, designer and troubleshooting guides are updated. Screenshots by `tools/docs/shots/about.py` into `docs/guides/img/about/` (sign-in on; the viewer picture masks `limit` as a labelled demo). The glossary, lint and coverage stages are described as the design says and marked not yet available.
- **Added:** About this page, step 6 ([CONTEXT_HELP.md](docs/architecture/CONTEXT_HELP.md)): pack lint warnings `DRS-2045` (a Sutra binds `F1`), `DRS-2046` (about `panels.<id>` matches no panel) and `DRS-2047` (a shown field has no glossary entry) in `sutra lint`, which fails on them with the new `--strict`; `expect.yaml` accepts `help: { coverage, about }`, `sutra test` prints `help coverage 5/5 (100%)` per Sutra and enforces a `tests/help-coverage.txt` floor (market-risk records 100%). Lint checks against a small `GlossaryLookup` that step 4's resolver can implement.
- **Added:** About this page, step 4 ([CONTEXT_HELP.md](docs/architecture/CONTEXT_HELP.md)): the glossary (layer 2). The explain answer gains `glossary[]`, one entry for exactly each field the page shows (strip, kv, tabs, metric, table columns), resolved kind glossary, then pack vocabulary by last name, then a derived kind's own formula (`DerivedKind`, through the new `SourcePlugin.describeField`), then a small core vocabulary in `drishti-rachana/.../about/vocabulary.yaml` (`GlossaryResolver`); a field hidden for the caller keeps its definition, marked `masked`, and never carries the meaning of a value; `?panel=` narrows the glossary and panel text. The console drawer gets the *What each number means* layer, the panel `?` opens a popover (its text, its fields, why it is empty or inferred, links), and strip, `kv` and table-header labels with an entry get a dotted underline and a tooltip on hover or focus (Enter opens the drawer at the entry, a phone tap pins it); the drawer's *Where next* links no longer repeat the kind ("F7 Desk DESK").
- **Added:** About this page, step 3 ([CONTEXT_HELP.md](docs/architecture/CONTEXT_HELP.md)): a pack's `config/about.yaml` (title, parameterised page text, glossary and vocabulary entries, panel text; strict parser, `DRS-2040` to `DRS-2044` with file, line and column, listed by `/api/v1/sutras/problems`), merged through `extends` (a child overrides key by key); the explain answer gains an `about` block (pack, kind title, the page text rendered with Rachana-EL over the document as the caller may see it, so a masked field reads the mask and nothing derived from it leaks, a failing expression reads `—`, text panel by panel) and the chosen Sutra's `pack`; written for market-risk `var` and genomics `variant` (generated from `tools/packgen/banking/about/` and `tools/packgen/genomics/about.yaml`).
- **Added:** About this page, step 2 ([CONTEXT_HELP.md](docs/architecture/CONTEXT_HELP.md)): the console's drawer on a view. `?` (outside a text field) or `F1` opens it, the new *About* button too; `Esc` closes it and the focus goes back; `F1` again goes to the screen guide as before. It shows the Sutra's description, where the data came from and how fresh it is (one source-health word), why the layout is what it is (Sutra and match trace, inferred panels, panels with no data, hidden fields, panels you may not open) and where next. Fetched from `GET /v/{kind}/{id}/about` (a partial over the server's explain endpoint) only when first opened; a live frame that moves the generation re-asks. A bottom sheet with a held focus on a phone, theme tokens only, no motion under reduced-motion. Tests: `test_about.py`, `test_about_browser.py` (against a live server), `test_contrast.py`.
- **Added:** About this page, step 1 ([CONTEXT_HELP.md](docs/architecture/CONTEXT_HELP.md)): `GET /api/v1/views/{kind}/{id}/explain` says where a page's data came from and how fresh it is (source, generation, business date, freshness, one-word connector health, linked-entity counts) and why it looks as it does (the Sutra chosen with its `where`, priority and description, the other candidates and what their `where` gave, inferred panels, panels with no data and why, panels that failed, fields hidden for the caller, panels the caller may not open, the function keys and panel kinds). It rebuilds the view for the caller so field masks and kind rights hold (a masked field's value is never in the answer; a `where` over a masked field reads `masked`), is computed only on request, cached per user for `drishti.explain.cache-ttl`, and adds `DRS-4006` (`?panel=` names no panel). `SutraMatcher.explain` gives the match trace; `match()` is unchanged; a Sutra's `description` is now kept at parse. The view's JSON is unchanged.
- **Docs:** [IDE_GUIDE.md](docs/guides/IDE_GUIDE.md): running and debugging the server in IntelliJ IDEA and the console in PyCharm (JDK and interpreter set-up for any vendor, run configurations, reloading changes, tests, common problems), linked from the README; QUICKSTART's IDE section moved there. Every document now states the Java requirement as 21 or newer (25 recommended); the console's live-server tests accept a JDK 21.
- **Changed:** Drishti now supports Java 21 and newer (25 stays recommended): the build targets Java 21 bytecode (`maven.compiler.release` 21), the enforcer rule is `[21,)` and says "Drishti needs Java 21 or newer (any vendor; 25 recommended)", `-XX:+UseCompactObjectHeaders` is used only on Java 25+ (the Docker image, the Windows start script and the docs; Java 21 refuses to start with it), and synchronized blocks that did I/O (the DuckDB file check, the Redis loader's journal and dictionary training, the Iceberg run cleanup) use a `ReentrantLock` so they do not pin virtual threads on Java 21. `NoBlockingUnderSynchronizedTest` fails if main code blocks inside `synchronized`. `tools/drill.sh` runs the tests on Java 21 as well. Load test of the connectors on both Javas: [PERFORMANCE.md](docs/admin/PERFORMANCE.md#java-21-and-virtual-thread-pinning).
- **Changed:** the build's Java check now says it needs Java 25 of any vendor (OpenJDK, Oracle, Temurin) and names the Java version and folder Maven actually runs on; it read "builds and runs on OpenJDK 25", which looked like a vendor check.

## 1.15.0 — The metric KPI tile, five developer guides, documentation harmonised (2026-10-04)
- **Docs:** [SUTRA_DEVELOPER_GUIDE.md](docs/guides/SUTRA_DEVELOPER_GUIDE.md) replaces `RACHANA_GUIDE.md`, `SUTRA_CLI.md` and the in-app `sutra-guide.md`: anatomy, matching and priority, labels, paths, source, row groups, inference, testing and CI, the workbench, a checklist and the common mistakes with their codes; the old help addresses redirect.
- **Docs:** [CONNECTOR_DEVELOPER_GUIDE.md](docs/connectors/CONNECTOR_DEVELOPER_GUIDE.md) replaces `PLUGIN_GUIDE.md` and `CONNECTOR_GUIDE.md`: which connector, the `SourcePlugin` SPI, testing with the testkit contracts, a worked teaching plugin (`docs/guides/examples/connector/`, built and contract-tested, not shipped) and one short section per connector; each connector's walk-through and worked examples moved into its own `*_CONNECTOR.md`, de-duplicated.
- **Docs:** [PACK_DEVELOPER_GUIDE.md](docs/guides/PACK_DEVELOPER_GUIDE.md) (build a pack end to end, with a help-desk pack) takes the developer half of `PACKS.md` and the in-app `build-a-pack` tutorial; `PACKS.md` keeps the user and admin content.
- **Docs:** [BUILD_WORKBENCH_TUTORIAL.md](docs/tutorials/06-design-a-screen.md): four complete projects with a picture of every step; fixed on the way: auto-design with a top-level figure that also occurs nested, bind on a kv panel writes columns, the YAML editor follows the canvas after typed edits, a rebase clears the base-moved flag.
- **Docs:** documentation integration: every link to a retired guide points at the new section, the help centre's `moved:` entries and Learn menu are updated, "twenty panel kinds" is twenty-one, no instruction mentions a Studio page.
- **Fixed:** after approving your own design its header no longer shows "base moved to ..." pointing at the version the design itself published (`DesignRebase` treats a design whose status is `live(vN)` for the latest version as up to date).
- **Docs:** [PANEL_DEVELOPER_GUIDE.md](docs/guides/PANELS.md#developing-a-panel-kind) (adding a panel kind end to end, worked through the real `metric` tile, with an ordered checklist and the guard tests) and [PANEL_KINDS.md](docs/guides/PANELS.md) (the catalogue of all twenty-one kinds, where F1 lands) replace PANELS.md, the in-app panel-kinds guide and DEVELOPER_GUIDE §5.4; screenshots are generated per guide by `tools/docs/screenshots.py --guide <name>` (the unreproducible `static/img/guide/kind-*.png` are gone).
- **Added:** `metric`, the 21st panel kind: a big-number KPI tile with one large formatted, toned figure (`value`, `label`, `fmt`, `tone`), an optional change beside it (`delta` with `deltaFmt` and `deltaTone`), a `unit` and a `caption`, and `source` like any panel; masked values show the mask, empty ones say so, it updates live, downloads as CSV, has an accessible name (`role=group`), a workbench palette entry (facts), inspector editors, an auto-design draft for a document's one headline measure and a suggestion for a single measure, and a `metric` example plus a tile in the all-panels showcase.

## 1.14.1 — LZ4 Delta tables, clean 413 for oversized requests, masking of copied values (2026-10-03)
- **Added:** Delta: LZ4 and LZ4_RAW Parquet pages are read by both engines. The native engine decodes LZ4_RAW (one block) and the deprecated LZ4 codec the way Arrow does (Hadoop framing when the lengths add up exactly, else one raw block) with lz4-java's safe decoder (`lz4-decoder: fast` for the fastest available); the `hadoop` engine reads LZ4 files, which parquet-java's Hadoop codec cannot when Arrow or pyarrow wrote them, through the native decoder (a per-file footer check; `lz4-via-native: false` turns it off). An LZ4 table is `UP` and lists and searches normally; only BROTLI, LZO and pages that do not decode are `DEGRADED`, naming the table, file and codec. Tests use real delta-rs/pyarrow fixtures (`plugins/drishti-plugin-delta/src/test/resources/lake-codecs`) compared value by value with the uncompressed copy on both engines. Supersedes the DATA-13 advice to rewrite LZ4 dates.

- Oversized builder requests get a clean `413` `DRS-5005` naming the size and the limit (declared or chunked, before the body is read; Tomcat drains a bounded remainder so there is no reset), the console says "This file is too large: X MB; the limit is Y MB"; new opt-in `drishti.security.mask-copies` scrubs exact copies of masked values inside other text of the same document, dotted `redact` entries mask by path, and the shipped setting also masks `lifecycle.timeline.description` (QA S2-08, S2-11).

## 1.14.0 — The Build workbench, and two rounds of adversarial QA closed (2026-10-03)
- **Fixed:** QA round 2 of the Build workbench (usability), every finding. Adding and removing panels is obvious: a **+ Add panel** button in the bar on every tab (a chooser of all 20 kinds), a filterable kind grid, a grip and resize handles, an *Add your first panel* call to action, Studio addresses open in Split view; panels are removed by a trash button, the inspector, a right-click menu or the palette, with a toast *Undo*. A design of your own kind has no false "not a valid kind" problem; a mistyped expression is a located Problem (code once) and the other panels still draw (the console cuts the failing panels out of a preview-only copy; the schema now carries per-kind options). The inspector edits every option: numbers, `expand` (number or all), selects for fixed sets (graph `layout`, tabs `layout`, surface `view`), `markers`, the table `pivot` object, the pivot `by` list and a tab `body`. DRS codes appear once on every Build page; a **Notes** tab (markdown preview, licence comment hidden); no sideways scroll at 390 px; a skip link and `G` then `C` to the canvas, Esc returns from the inspector; one live region; F1 and links send a pending edit first; in-page dialogs instead of `confirm()`/`prompt()`; unknown `?example=`/`?design=`/`?sutra=` say so; palette `go to sample` by file name, plus Auto-design, Split, Phone, Theme, Bind and Remove; guide pictures regenerated, `docs/qa/2026-10-03/ux/FINDINGS.md` marks each row.
- **Docs:** `docs/architecture/HOW_IT_FITS.md`, "How Drishti fits together": how kinds, mnemonics, packs, connectors, Sutras, links and roles refer to each other, and the runtime path of `TRD MX-20000001` from the command box through the console, server, source router, Sutra matcher, layout, binder and ViewModel to the live patches, with real files and outputs, a second example in the genomics pack, the no-Sutra case, how the Build workbench adds a screen, and a where-to-change-what table; linked from the docs map, ARCHITECTURE, QUICKSTART and Help → Developers.
- **Tests:** jqwik removed; property tests are plain JUnit with seeded generators (`-Ddrishti.test.seed` overrides the base seed; failures print case index, seed and input).
- **Fixed:** QA round 2, governance and safety. Development file binding (S2-01, S2-05, S2-06, S2-07, S2-13) writes only to your own folder under `drishti.builder.dev-dir`, which must lie outside the loaded Sutra directories, so *Save to file* can no longer make a Sutra live without review; symbolic links are never followed, writes go through an exclusively created temporary file, bad names and oversize bodies are clean `400`/`413` problems, `sync` is a POST, and the binding info shows no absolute path to non-administrators.
- **Fixed:** a Design publishes `name@(latest+1)` and never rewrites a version; an unrelated Design over an existing `name@version` (a pack's included) is refused at propose time with `DRS-2028` (M-1). When the base Sutra gets a newer version the Design flags it and *Rebase* replays its operations on it, reporting what cannot be replayed (M-2).
- **Fixed:** limits. The synthetic sampler is bounded by `drishti.builder.sample-max-*` and clamps with a problem (S2-02); Sutra, notes and tests count in the design and user size and have their own caps (S2-03); a long text no longer reads as "nested too deep" (S2-08); scratch designs have a cap of their own, never fill the named quota, are evicted oldest first and have a bulk delete (UX-05); the console enforces body limits while streaming chunked bodies and answers an over-limit import with 413 (S2-09).
- **Fixed:** `GET /studio?...` no longer creates a design: it shows a confirmation whose button POSTs (S2-04). Proposal evidence and share links show reference sample names as `kind (reference)` to viewers who may not open the kind, and the notes only to the author and approvers (S2-10); four-eyes compares canonical user names (S2-14); the stateless builder endpoints and `sutra design --kind` validate `kind` (L-7); the `sutra` CLI keeps the input size limit, stays inside its input and never prints a stack trace (S2-12); masking of free text is documented precisely (S2-11).
- **Fixed:** QA round 2, data and engine (2026-10-03). Shape: bounds beyond 2^53 are exact and non-finite numbers carry none, sparse optional sections are no longer read as maps, `markdown` accepts `source:`. Pack fragments: notes live in `NOTES.md` (no growth on re-import) and sample names keep their Unicode. `sutra test`: the JUnit file is always written, invalid/BOM/empty samples are failed cases, `expect.yaml` typos fail, `.jsonl` is read. Synthetic samples honour `pattern`, `oneOf`, `patternProperties`, `prefixItems` and recursion limits, and refuse dangling `$ref`. Auto-design: monthly series, tenor grids, single-root trees, year/version/number codes not formatted as amounts, no always-empty links panel, stable key order, gauges for shares (`fraction-names`), tabs for short four-field lists. Operations refuse lists/mappings for single-value options and `setStrip` keeps its comments. Tree tables skip non-record children; oversize uploads answer `413`; messages say MiB, a text `baseRev` says "whole number", expression problems state their code once.
- **Added:** Build workbench step 8, ship and scale. **Propose with evidence:** the workbench's *Submit for review* (`POST /api/v1/builder/designs/{id}/propose`) sends the check matrix, the sample names, the notes and the design's identity with the proposal; reviewers see them on `/build/reviews/{id}`; approval makes the design `live(vN)` (shown beside its title and on *My designs*), rejection returns it to `draft`.
- **Added:** pack fragments: *Ship → Export as a pack fragment* downloads a zip (`pack.yaml` stub with the kind, a mnemonic and the id field, the Sutra, `tests/<sutra>/*.json` + `expect.yaml`, three samples, a README) that loads as a pack and passes `sutra test`; *New screen → Or import a pack* turns a pack folder or zip into designs, one per Sutra, with its samples and notes.
- **Added:** development file binding (`drishti.builder.file-binding`, default off): a design bound to a file under `drishti.rachana.dirs` saves by writing the file, and an edit made in an IDE comes back into the design (polled every few seconds); a clash with unsynced edits is `409 DRS-5007`.
- **Added:** read-only share links (*Ship → Create a read-only link*, `/build/d/{id}?share=token`): the Sutra, the operations and the sample names, never sample contents or notes; the recipient previews against their own JSON or a stored entity they may open; revocable at once.
- **Added:** the screen designer guide is finished: an end-to-end walkthrough (folder, design, check, propose, approve, live), pack fragments, sharing, the command line and CI, file binding, troubleshooting, limits and a keyboard reference for shipping, with eight new screenshots (`tools/docs/screenshots.py` now also runs against a scratch server with saving and binding on).
- **Fixed:** the workbench reloaded an empty Sutra after a 409 or an auto-design (it read `yaml` from a design that answers `sutra`); and `tools/docs/screenshots.py` never started its scratch servers because an HTTP error counted as "nothing listens".
- **Fixed:** in a narrow `status` or `kv` panel two labels ran into each other ("Confirmation" into "Clearing"): labels now wrap (`overflow-wrap`), and a very narrow panel (under 15rem) stacks them one per row; pinned by `test_panel_polish_browser.py` at four widths.
- **Fixed:** a waterfall's value labels overlapped at small widths: the labels now fit their columns when they can, turn to read upward (with room made above the bars) when the columns are narrow, and are left out when not even that fits; the chart is laid out again on resize (`drishtiCharts.labelFit`).
- **Fixed:** the all-panels showcase (and `linked-sources`) opened as a copy with "Problems 2" although nothing was wrong. Causes: the light YAML check read a flow map written over several lines (`- { id: explain, ...,` then `rows: ...}`) as cut at the line end and said `rows` was missing (`studio-yaml.js` now joins the continuation lines); the Problems tab flagged `children: "@.children"` of a tree table as present in only some samples (a tree's `children` is rare by nature, now skipped); and the schema typed `limit` as a whole number for every panel kind though an area's is an expression (`exposure-profile` was told "limit is a whole number"). Every example now opens with an empty Problems tab (`test_showcase_clean_browser.py`; kind and entity messages of the banking packs aside).
- **Fixed:** a `source:` link to an entity no store holds said "waiting for NOPE-1" like one still being fetched; it now says "NOPE-1 not found" (`SourceRouter.fetchAll` reports the refs no source holds; "waiting for" stays for a source that failed or timed out).
- **Fixed:** `test_live_tabs_browser.py[leader]` was timing-sensitive (failed once in a full run): the election, the single shared connection and the takeover after the leader closes are now waited for as conditions.
- **Changed:** Build workbench step 7, Studio folded in: Sutra Studio has no page of its own any more. Every `/studio` address (`?example=`, `?sutra=name@v`, `?kind=&id=`, `?design=&sample=`, `?build=1`) answers a 302 into the workbench (`/build/d/{id}`, a new design started for it: a scratch copy of the showcase, a copy of the example, a design that edits that Sutra with Studio's test entities carried over as stored-entity samples, or a stored entity as the sample), and `/studio/reviews[/{id}]` into `/build/reviews[/{id}]`. The workbench has Studio's keys (Ctrl+S saves or says why not, Ctrl+Enter previews), a File menu (open a `.yaml` or `.json`, or an example as a copy), Save or Submit for review under the same rights as before (designing open to everyone, saving needs the author right), a **command palette** (Ctrl+K: add or go to a panel, next sample, run the check, switch tab, undo, save, guide), and a **Versions** tab that diffs the design against the Sutra it came from or any earlier step of its log and restores one as an operation (`GET /api/v1/builder/designs/{id}/versions[/{n}]`). The Build menu is Create, Govern (Reviews, with the number waiting) and Learn; the *Sutra Studio* tutorial redirects to the screen designer guide, which gains four chapters.
- **Added:** headless Sutra CLI: `java -jar drishti-server-*-exec.jar sutra lint|test|shape|design|preview <path>` runs the workbench engine without a web server (exit codes 0/1/2, `--junit` XML, `--out` HTML snapshots), with the `packs/<p>/tests/<sutra>/*.json` + `expect.yaml` convention (`noErrors`, `nonEmpty`, per-sample overrides); `SutraCliTest` runs `sutra test` over every shipped pack's tests and the ten examples (docs/guides/SUTRA_CLI.md).
- **Fixed:** the shape extractor wrote a schema that rejected its own sample when a list of records held `null` items (`{"c":{"name":[null,{"x y":false}],"x y":204}}`): the nulls were folded into the record, so the record turned nullable while the tree's `items` `$ref` did not allow null. The null items now belong to the list (`items` is `anyOf` the record or null) and the record keeps its own nullability; found by `ShapePropertyTest`, pinned by `ShapeSelfValidationTest`.
- **Fixed:** the linked-sources example previewed with "waiting for NS-SUMMIT-NY" in tests: a pasted document already fetches its `source:` entities like a stored one (same pipeline, link budget and open-rights); the entities were simply not held because the banking packs were not enabled. `ExamplePreviewTest` runs every example and a Studio paste of linked-sources with the packs enabled and no longer tolerates "waiting for".
- **Added:** Build workbench step 6, the visual canvas: a design's page (`/build/d/{id}`) is now the workbench. Data on the left (samples, the 20-kind palette, the shape tree whose fields can be dragged), **Design / YAML / Summary** in the middle (split view), **Inspector / Problems / Tests** on the right, a status bar with the sample switcher, desktop or phone width, theme, the check result, the revision, undo and redo and *Preview with a file…*. The Design tab draws the real preview with an editing layer: click to select a panel, the title or the strip; drag a panel by its heading to move it (a bar shows where it lands), drag its edges to size it on the 12-column grid, Delete removes it; drag a palette kind to add a panel, drag a shape field to bind it to a panel or to get ranked panel kinds with reasons; every change is one operation sent with its base revision (a stale one is a 409, reloaded and said). The inspector is generated from the Rachana schema (required options first, selects, switches, expression fields that complete `$.`/`@.` paths and functions, reorderable lists). Typing in the YAML tab is a Text operation; Problems lists YAML, operation (DRS-502x), binding and check problems with a jump to each; the Tests tab re-checks every sample 0.8 s after the last change. Everything has a keyboard path (arrows select, Alt+arrows move, Shift+arrows size, `N` adds, `B` binds, Ctrl+Z/Ctrl+Shift+Z) announced in a live region; the move/size keys are one shared module (`build/grid-keys.js`) used by layout mode too. New guide `docs/guides/SCREEN_DESIGNER.md` with screenshots made by `tools/docs/screenshots.py` (F1 on any Build page, *Build → Learn*, Help → Examples "Open in the workbench"); the help centre serves pictures under `docs/` at `/help/files/`.
- **Added:** Build workbench step 4, Designs: your work on a screen is kept on the server as a *design*, owned by you alone (others get `404 DRS-5006`): `/api/v1/builder/designs` (create, list, read, rename, delete, duplicate; samples as files, references to stored entities re-read with your current rights and masks, or synthetic documents generated from a JSON Schema; `/shape`, `/preview?sample=`, `/autodesign`), in files (`data/designs/<user>/<id>/`, owner-only permissions) or the identity database (`drishti.builder.designs.store`), with quotas (50 designs, 50 samples and 25 MB each, 250 MB in all; limits answer `413 DRS-5005`) and expiry (scratch 1 day, named 90 days, warning from 75; a sweeper deletes them). Console: **Build → New screen** (`/build/new`: files, a folder, a schema, stored entities, examples as your own copies, an existing Sutra; start from auto-design, that Sutra or an empty one), **My designs** (`/build`) and a design's page (`/build/d/{id}`: data and shape tree, Sutra, preview with a sample switcher, Open in Studio). Replaces the console's in-memory sample sets (`builder.ttl_hours` is gone); `/build/shape` redirects to `/build/new#files`. Studio, `/studio?example=` and the help centre examples are unchanged.
- **Changed:** designing and trying are open to every signed-in user; only deploying stays permission-based. Studio, its preview/check/inference against a pasted document, File → Open and the examples, and the Screen Builder (`/build/shape`, `POST /api/v1/builder/shape`, `/design`, `/suggest`) no longer need the `author` power or the right to the pasted document's kind (it is a label, not a permission). Saving, proposing, approving and deleting are unchanged (403 without the right); Studio disables Save/Propose with "Designing is open to everyone; saving needs the author right". Previewing a stored entity (kind + id) still requires the right to its kind and the field masks.
